kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.fragments

import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.provider.Settings
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.CompoundButton
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.services.GameVpnService
import com.fspedx.booster.services.OverlayService
import com.fspedx.booster.services.SessionRecorderService
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.backup.BackupManager
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.RefreshRateUtils
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.ThermalUtils
import com.google.android.material.switchmaterial.SwitchMaterial
import java.io.File

class FeaturesFragment : Fragment() {

    // UI elements
    private lateinit var recyclerView: RecyclerView
    private lateinit var adapter: FeatureAdapter

    private val prefs by lazy { (requireContext().applicationContext as FSPEDXApplication).prefs }

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View? {
        return inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_features, container, false)
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        recyclerView = view.findViewById(R.id.featuresRecyclerView)
        recyclerView.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(requireContext())

        // Build feature list
        val features = buildFeatureList()
        adapter = FeatureAdapter(features)
        recyclerView.adapter = adapter
    }

    private fun buildFeatureList(): List<FeatureItem> {
        return listOf(
            // ===== SECTION A: PERFORMANCE ENGINE =====
            FeatureItem.Section("PERFORMANCE ENGINE"),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "FPS / Temp Overlay",
                "Show live FPS and battery temperature",
                R.drawable.ic_speed,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_FPS_OVERLAY, true),
                Constants.KEY_FPS_OVERLAY
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Refresh Rate Switch",
                "Drop 120Hz → 60Hz for stable gaming",
                R.drawable.ic_refresh,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_REFRESH_RATE_SWITCH, true),
                Constants.KEY_REFRESH_RATE_SWITCH
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Thermal Sentinel",
                "Auto-throttle if temp > 45°C",
                R.drawable.ic_thermostat,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_THERMAL_SENTINEL, true),
                Constants.KEY_THERMAL_SENTINEL
            ),
            FeatureItem.RadioGroup(
                "Profile Selector",
                listOf("Turbo", "Eco"),
                if (prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_TURBO_MODE, true)) 0 else 1
            ),
            FeatureItem.Button(
                "Safe Cache Cleaner",
                "Clear junk from other apps (skips PES)",
                R.drawable.ic_delete_sweep
            ) { cleanCache() },

            // ===== SECTION B: NETWORK TUNNELING =====
            FeatureItem.Section("NETWORK TUNNELING"),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Wi-Fi Low-Latency Lock",
                "Prioritize game packets over battery",
                R.drawable.ic_wifi,

            ),

                R.drawable.ic_dns,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_SMART_DNS, true),
                Constants.KEY_SMART_DNS
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Dual-Channel Rescue",
                "Use WiFi + Mobile Data simultaneously",
                R.drawable.ic_signal_cellular_alt,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_DUAL_CHANNEL, false),
                Constants.KEY_DUAL_CHANNEL
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Local VPN Tunnel",
                "Route only PES traffic through our tunnel",
                R.drawable.ic_vpn_lock,

                "Access controls without leaving your game",
                R.drawable.ic_gesture_pill,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_FLOATING_PILL, true),
                Constants.KEY_FLOATING_PILL
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Side Pull-Out Table",
                "Swipe from left for live stats dashboard",
                R.drawable.ic_table_rows,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_SIDE_PANEL, true),
                Constants.KEY_SIDE_PANEL
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Aiming Crosshair",
                "Drag an overlay guide for precise passes/shots",
                R.drawable.ic_crosshair,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_CROSSHAIR, false),
                Constants.KEY_CROSSHAIR
            ),

            // ===== SECTION D: SYSTEM & PROTECTION =====
            FeatureItem.Section("SYSTEM & PROTECTION"),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Auto-Launch Trigger",
                "Auto-enable boosters when PES starts",
                R.drawable.ic_play_circle,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_AUTO_LAUNCH, true),
                Constants.KEY_AUTO_LAUNCH
            ),
            FeatureItem.Toggle(
                "Smart DND",
                "Mute calls and notifications during matches",
                R.drawable.ic_volume_off,
                prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_DND_MODE, true),
                Constants.KEY_DND_MODE
            ),
            FeatureItem.Button(
                "App Whitelist",
                "Protect apps from being killed by RAM booster",
                R.drawable.ic_shield
            ) { showWhitelistDialog() },
            FeatureItem.Button(
                "Backup & Restore",
                "Export/Import settings and heatmap data",
                R.drawable.ic_backup
            ) { showBackupDialog() },
        )
    }

    // ============ ACTION HANDLERS ============

    private fun cleanCache() {
        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Scanning cache...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        // Implement safe cache cleaning
        // Skipping PES package (com.konami.pes)
        // This is a placeholder - actual implementation in RamCleaner
    }

    private fun runPingTest() {
        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Running ping test...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        // Delegate to HomeFragment or NetworkUtils
    }

    private fun showWhitelistDialog() {
        val dialogView = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.dialog_whitelist, null)
        AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
            .setTitle("Protected Apps")
            .setView(dialogView)
            .setPositiveButton("Add") { _, _ ->
                // Open system app picker
                val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_SETTINGS)
                startActivity(intent)
            }
            .setNegativeButton("Close", null)
            .show()
    }

    private fun showBackupDialog() {
        AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
            .setTitle("Backup & Restore")
            .setMessage("Export settings to file?")
            .setPositiveButton("Export") { _, _ ->
                val success = BackupManager.exportSettings(requireContext())
                if (success) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Settings exported to Downloads", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Export failed", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
            .setNegativeButton("Import", { _, _ ->
                // Open file picker
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT)
                intent.type = "application/json"
                startActivityForResult(intent, 1001)
            })
            .setNeutralButton("Cancel", null)
            .show()
    }

    // ============ TOGGLE LISTENER ============

    inner class FeatureAdapter(private val items: List<FeatureItem>) :
        RecyclerView.Adapter<RecyclerView.ViewHolder>() {

        companion object {
            private const val TYPE_SECTION = 0
            private const val TYPE_TOGGLE = 1
            private const val TYPE_RADIO = 2
            private const val TYPE_BUTTON = 3
        }

        override fun getItemViewType(position: Int): Int {
            return when (items[position]) {
                is FeatureItem.Section -> TYPE_SECTION
                is FeatureItem.Toggle -> TYPE_TOGGLE
                is FeatureItem.RadioGroup -> TYPE_RADIO
                is FeatureItem.Button -> TYPE_BUTTON
            }
        }

        override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
            val inflater = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            return when (viewType) {
                TYPE_SECTION -> {
                    val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_feature_section, parent, false)
                    SectionViewHolder(view)
                }
                TYPE_TOGGLE -> {
                    val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_feature_toggle, parent, false)
                    ToggleViewHolder(view)
                }
                TYPE_RADIO -> {
                    val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_feature_radio, parent, false)
                    RadioViewHolder(view)
                }
                else -> {
                    val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_feature_button, parent, false)
                    ButtonViewHolder(view)
                }
            }
        }

        override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, position: Int) {
            when (holder) {
                is SectionViewHolder -> {
                    val item = items[position] as FeatureItem.Section
                    holder.bind(item)
                }
                is ToggleViewHolder -> {
                    val item = items[position] as FeatureItem.Toggle
                    holder.bind(item)
                }
                is RadioViewHolder -> {
                    val item = items[position] as FeatureItem.RadioGroup
                    holder.bind(item)
                }
                is ButtonViewHolder -> {
                    val item = items[position] as FeatureItem.Button
                    holder.bind(item)
                }
            }
        }

        override fun getItemCount(): Int = items.size

        // ===== VIEW HOLDERS =====

        inner class SectionViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
            private val tvSection = view.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.tvSectionTitle)

            fun bind(item: FeatureItem.Section) {
                tvSection.text = item.title
            }
        }

        inner class ToggleViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
            private val tvTitle = view.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.tvToggleTitle)
            private val tvDesc = view.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.tvToggleDesc)
            private val switch = view.findViewById<SwitchMaterial>(R.id.switchToggle)

            fun bind(item: FeatureItem.Toggle) {
                tvTitle.text = item.title
                tvDesc.text = item.description
                switch.isChecked = item.isChecked
                switch.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, isChecked ->
                    prefs.edit().putBoolean(item.prefKey, isChecked).apply()
                    // Handle specific toggle actions
                    when (item.prefKey) {
                        Constants.KEY_FPS_OVERLAY -> {
                            if (isChecked) OverlayService.start(requireContext())
                            else OverlayService.stop(requireContext())
                        }
                        Constants.KEY_VPN_TUNNEL -> {
                            if (isChecked) {
                                val intent = android.net.VpnService.prepare(requireContext())
                                if (intent != null) {
                                    startActivity(intent)
                                } else {
                                    GameVpnService.start(requireContext())
                                }
                            } else {
                                GameVpnService.stop(requireContext())
                            }
                        }
                        Constants.KEY_REFRESH_RATE_SWITCH -> {
                            if (isChecked) RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(requireContext(), 60)
                            else RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(requireContext(), 120)
                        }
                        Constants.KEY_FLOATING_PILL -> {
                            if (isChecked) OverlayService.showFloatingPill(requireContext())
                            else OverlayService.hideFloatingPill(requireContext())
                        }
                        // Add more toggle handlers as needed
                    }
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "${item.title}: ${if (isChecked) "ON" else "OFF"}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }

        inner class RadioViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
            private val tvTitle = view.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.tvRadioTitle)
            private val radioGroup = view.findViewById<com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButtonToggleGroup>(R.id.radioGroup)

            fun bind(item: FeatureItem.RadioGroup) {
                tvTitle.text = item.title
                // Set selection
                when (item.defaultSelected) {
                    0 -> radioGroup.check(R.id.radioTurbo)
                    1 -> radioGroup.check(R.id.radioEco)
                }
                radioGroup.addOnButtonCheckedListener { _, checkedId, isChecked ->
                    if (isChecked) {
                        val isTurbo = checkedId == R.id.radioTurbo
                        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_TURBO_MODE, isTurbo).apply()
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Profile: ${if (isTurbo) "Turbo" else "Eco"}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        inner class ButtonViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
            private val btn = view.findViewById<android.widget.Button>(R.id.btnFeatureAction)
            private val tvDesc = view.findViewById<android.widget.TextView>(R.id.tvButtonDesc)

            fun bind(item: FeatureItem.Button) {
                btn.text = item.title
                tvDesc.text = item.description
                btn.setOnClickListener { item.action.invoke() }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============ DATA CLASSES FOR FEATURE ITEMS ============

sealed class FeatureItem {
    data class Section(val title: String) : FeatureItem()
    data class Toggle(
        val title: String,
        val description: String,
        val iconRes: Int,
        val isChecked: Boolean,
        val prefKey: String
    ) : FeatureItem()

    data class RadioGroup(
        val title: String,
        val options: List<String>,
        val defaultSelected: Int
    ) : FeatureItem()

    data class Button(
        val title: String,
        val description: String,
        val iconRes: Int,
        val action: () -> Unit
    ) : FeatureItem()
}

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.fragments

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageView
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.viewpager2.adapter.FragmentStateAdapter
import androidx.viewpager2.widget.ViewPager2
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.ml.KMeansCluster
import com.fspedx.booster.viewmodels.AnalyticsViewModel
import com.github.mikephil.charting.charts.LineChart
import com.github.mikephil.charting.data.Entry
import com.github.mikephil.charting.data.LineData
import com.github.mikephil.charting.data.LineDataSet
import com.google.android.material.tabs.TabLayout
import com.google.android.material.tabs.TabLayoutMediator

class AnalyticsFragment : Fragment() {

    private lateinit var viewModel: AnalyticsViewModel
    private lateinit var viewPager: ViewPager2
    private lateinit var tabLayout: TabLayout

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View? {
        return inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_analytics, container, false)
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        viewModel = ViewModelProvider(this)[AnalyticsViewModel::class.java]

        viewPager = view.findViewById(R.id.analyticsViewPager)
        tabLayout = view.findViewById(R.id.analyticsTabLayout)

        // Set up ViewPager with 3 tabs
        val adapter = AnalyticsPagerAdapter(this)
        viewPager.adapter = adapter

        TabLayoutMediator(tabLayout, viewPager) { tab, position ->
            when (position) {
                0 -> tab.text = "Heatmap"
                1 -> tab.text = "Mis-taps"
                2 -> tab.text = "Timeline"
            }
        }.attach()
    }

    // ===== INNER FRAGMENTS FOR TABS =====

    class HeatmapFragment : Fragment() {
        override fun onCreateView(
            inflater: LayoutInflater,
            container: ViewGroup?,
            savedInstanceState: Bundle?
        ): View? {
            val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_heatmap, container, false)
            
            val imageView = view.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.heatmapImageView)
            val tvCentroids = view.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvCentroids)
            val btnSuggest = view.findViewById<android.widget.Button>(R.id.btnSuggestPositions)

            // TODO: Load heatmap image from database
            // For now, show placeholder
            // imageView.setImageResource(R.drawable.default_controller)

            btnSuggest.setOnClickListener {
                // Run K-Means on collected touch data
                val touches = emptyList<KMeansCluster.TouchPoint>() // TODO: Load from DB
                val centroids = KMeansCluster.findCentroids(touches, 4) // 4 buttons: Pass, Shoot, Sprint, Direction
                tvCentroids.text = "Suggested positions:\n${centroids.joinToString("\n") { "(${it.x}, ${it.y})" }}"
            }

            return view
        }
    }

    class MisTapFragment : Fragment() {
        override fun onCreateView(
            inflater: LayoutInflater,
            container: ViewGroup?,
            savedInstanceState: Bundle?
        ): View? {
            val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_mistaps, container, false)
            
            val tvMisTaps = view.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvMisTapCount)
            val btnTrain = view.findViewById<android.widget.Button>(R.id.btnVibrationTraining)

            // TODO: Load mis-tap data from database
            // tvMisTaps.text = "Mis-taps detected: 23"

            btnTrain.setOnClickListener {
                // Toggle Vibration Training feature
                android.widget.Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "Vibration Training: ON", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }

            return view
        }
    }

    class TimelineFragment : Fragment() {
        override fun onCreateView(
            inflater: LayoutInflater,
            container: ViewGroup?,
            savedInstanceState: Bundle?
        ): View? {
            val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_timeline, container, false)
            
            val lineChart = view.findViewById<LineChart>(R.id.lagTimelineChart)
            
            // Sample data - replace with actual from database
            val entries = listOf(
                Entry(0f, 40f),
                Entry(2f, 45f),
                Entry(4f, 120f),
                Entry(6f, 38f),
                Entry(8f, 42f),
                Entry(10f, 300f),
                Entry(12f, 35f)
            )

            val dataSet = LineDataSet(entries, "Ping (ms)")
            dataSet.color = resources.getColor(R.color.primary_cyan, null)
            dataSet.setCircleColor(resources.getColor(R.color.primary_cyan, null))
            dataSet.lineWidth = 2f
            dataSet.circleRadius = 4f
            dataSet.valueTextColor = resources.getColor(R.color.secondary_text, null)
            
            val lineData = LineData(dataSet)
            lineChart.data = lineData
            
            lineChart.setPinchZoom(true)
            lineChart.animateX(1000)
            lineChart.invalidate()

            return view
        }
    }

    // ===== VIEWPAGER ADAPTER =====

    inner class AnalyticsPagerAdapter(fragment: Fragment) : FragmentStateAdapter(fragment) {
        override fun getItemCount(): Int = 3

        override fun createFragment(position: Int): Fragment {
            return when (position) {
                0 -> HeatmapFragment()
                1 -> MisTapFragment()
                2 -> TimelineFragment()
                else -> HeatmapFragment()
            }
        }
    }
}

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.fragments

import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Environment
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageView
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.GridLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import java.io.File

class GalleryFragment : Fragment() {

    private lateinit var recyclerView: RecyclerView
    private lateinit var tvEmpty: TextView

    override fun onCreateView(
        inflater: LayoutInflater,
        container: ViewGroup?,
        savedInstanceState: Bundle?
    ): View? {
        return inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_gallery, container, false)
    }

    override fun onViewCreated(view: View, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState)

        recyclerView = view.findViewById(R.id.galleryRecyclerView)
        tvEmpty = view.findViewById(R.id.tvEmpty)

        recyclerView.layoutManager = GridLayoutManager(requireContext(), 2)

        // Load recordings from storage
        val recordingsDir = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES)
            ?.resolve(Constants.RECORDINGS_DIR)
        
        val videoFiles = recordingsDir?.listFiles { file ->
            file.extension in listOf("mp4", "mkv", "3gp")
        } ?: emptyArray()

        if (videoFiles.isEmpty()) {
            tvEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            recyclerView.visibility = View.GONE
        } else {
            tvEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
            recyclerView.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            val adapter = ReplayGridAdapter(videoFiles.toList())
            recyclerView.adapter = adapter
        }
    }

    // ===== INNER ADAPTER =====

    inner class ReplayGridAdapter(private val files: List<File>) :
        RecyclerView.Adapter<ReplayGridAdapter.ViewHolder>() {

        class ViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
            val thumbnail: ImageView = view.findViewById(R.id.ivThumbnail)
            val tvFileName: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvFileName)
        }

        override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ViewHolder {
            val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
                .inflate(R.layout.item_replay_thumbnail, parent, false)
            return ViewHolder(view)
        }

        override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ViewHolder, position: Int) {
            val file = files[position]
            holder.tvFileName.text = file.name.replace(".mp4", "").replace(".mkv", "")
            
            // TODO: Generate thumbnail from video file
            // For now, use a placeholder
            holder.thumbnail.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_play_circle)

            holder.itemView.setOnClickListener {
                // TODO: Play video with heatmap overlay
                android.widget.Toast.makeText(
                    requireContext(),
                    "Playing: ${file.name}",
                    android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
                ).show()
            }
        }

        override fun getItemCount(): Int = files.size
    }
}

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.adapters

import android.content.Context
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageView
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import androidx.fragment.app.FragmentActivity
import androidx.viewpager2.adapter.FragmentStateAdapter
import com.fspedx.booster.R

/**
 * Adapter for the 4-step onboarding permission wizard.
 * Each step explains a permission and guides the user to enable it.
 */
class OnboardingPagerAdapter(fragmentActivity: FragmentActivity) :
    FragmentStateAdapter(fragmentActivity) {

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = 4

    override fun createFragment(position: Int): Fragment {
        return OnboardingStepFragment.newInstance(position)
    }

    /**
     * Inner Fragment for each onboarding step.
     * Uses position to determine which step to display.
     */
    class OnboardingStepFragment : Fragment() {

        companion object {
            private const val ARG_POSITION = "position"

            fun newInstance(position: Int): OnboardingStepFragment {
                val fragment = OnboardingStepFragment()
                val args = Bundle()
                args.putInt(ARG_POSITION, position)
                fragment.arguments = args
                return fragment
            }
        }

        override fun onCreateView(
            inflater: LayoutInflater,
            container: ViewGroup?,
            savedInstanceState: Bundle?
        ): View? {
            val position = arguments?.getInt(ARG_POSITION) ?: 0
            val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_onboarding_step, container, false)

            val ivIcon = view.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.ivStepIcon)
            val tvTitle = view.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvStepTitle)
            val tvDesc = view.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvStepDescription)

            when (position) {
                0 -> {
                    ivIcon.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_visibility)
                    tvTitle.text = "Show Live FPS & Temp"
                    tvDesc.text = "Allow FSPEDX to draw over other apps so you can see your frame rate and battery temperature while playing PES."
                }
                1 -> {
                    ivIcon.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_touch_app)
                    tvTitle.text = "Record Taps for Heatmaps"
                    tvDesc.text = "Enable Accessibility so FSPEDX can capture where you tap on the screen. This data generates heatmaps and suggests better button positions."
                }
                2 -> {
                    ivIcon.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_vpn_lock)
                    tvTitle.text = "Zero-Lag Network Tunnel"
                    tvDesc.text = "Setup the VPN tunnel to route only PES traffic through our optimized path. This reduces ping and prioritizes your game packets."
                }
                3 -> {
                    ivIcon.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_do_not_disturb)
                    tvTitle.text = "Block Calls & Notifications"
                    tvDesc.text = "Allow DND mode so you never get interrupted by WhatsApp calls or notifications during a crucial match moment."
                }
            }

            return view
        }
    }
}

package com.fspedx.booster.ui.adapters

import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageView
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.fspedx.booster.R

/**
 * Adapter for displaying the list of apps protected from RAM killing.
 * Shows app icon, name, and a remove button.
 */
class WhitelistAdapter(
    private val packageNames: MutableSet<String>,
    private val onRemove: (String) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<WhitelistAdapter.ViewHolder>() {

    private val packageManager: PackageManager by lazy {
        // Pass context from the fragment/activity later
        // For now, we need context, but we don't have it in constructor.
        // We'll use a lazy delegate or pass it as a param.
        // Let's modify to take context.
        throw IllegalStateException("PackageManager not initialized. Use constructor with context.")
    }

    // We'll use a proper constructor
    constructor(
        context: android.content.Context,
        packageNames: MutableSet<String>,
        onRemove: (String) -> Unit
    ) : this(packageNames, onRemove) {
        this.packageManager = context.packageManager
    }

    class ViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
        val ivIcon: ImageView = view.findViewById(R.id.ivAppIcon)
        val tvName: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvAppName)
        val btnRemove: ImageView = view.findViewById(R.id.btnRemove)
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            .inflate(R.layout.item_whitelist_app, parent, false)
        return ViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        val packageName = packageNames.elementAt(position)
        
        try {
            val appInfo = packageManager.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
            holder.ivIcon.setImageDrawable(appInfo.loadIcon(packageManager))
            holder.tvName.text = packageManager.getApplicationLabel(appInfo)
        } catch (e: PackageManager.NameNotFoundException) {
            holder.ivIcon.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_app_placeholder)
            holder.tvName.text = packageName
        }

        holder.btnRemove.setOnClickListener {
            onRemove(packageName)
        }
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = packageNames.size

    /**
     * Update the dataset with a new set.
     */
    fun updateData(newSet: Set<String>) {
        packageNames.clear()
        packageNames.addAll(newSet)
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}

/**

class ReplayGridAdapter(
    private val files: List<File>,
    private val onItemClick: (File) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<ReplayGridAdapter.ViewHolder>() {

    class ViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
        val thumbnail: ImageView = view.findViewById(R.id.ivThumbnail)
        val tvFileName: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvFileName)
        val tvDuration: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvDuration)
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): ViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            .inflate(R.layout.item_replay_thumbnail, parent, false)
        return ViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: ViewHolder, position: Int) {

        val fileName = file.name.replace(".mp4", "").replace(".mkv", "").replace(".3gp", "")

        // For now, use placeholder icon

        // Try to get video duration (optional)

        holder.tvDuration.visibility = View.GONE // Hide if not implemented

        holder.itemView.setOnClickListener {
            onItemClick(file)
        }
    }

    override fun getItemCount(): Int = files.size
}

Overlay 2: Draggable quick-pill with Boost and Record buttons.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.widget.ImageButton
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import androidx.constraintlayout.widget.ConstraintLayout
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants

/**
 * Draggable floating pill that sits on top of games.
 * Contains Boost and Record quick-action buttons.
 * Managed by OverlayService.
 */
@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class FloatingPillView(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null,
    defStyleAttr: Int = 0
) : ConstraintLayout(context, attrs, defStyleAttr) {

    // Buttons
    private lateinit var btnBoost: ImageButton
    private lateinit var btnRecord: ImageButton
    
    // Callbacks
    var onBoostClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onRecordClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onDismiss: (() -> Unit)? = null

    // Dragging state
    private var dX = 0f
    private var dY = 0f
    private var lastX = 0f
    private var lastY = 0f
    private var isDragging = false
    private val touchSlop = 10f // Minimum movement to detect drag

    init {
        inflate(context, R.layout.view_floating_pill, this)
        
        btnBoost = findViewById(R.id.btnPillBoost)
        btnRecord = findViewById(R.id.btnPillRecord)
        
        // Close button (X) to dismiss the pill
        val btnClose = findViewById<ImageButton>(R.id.btnPillClose)
        
        btnBoost.setOnClickListener {
            onBoostClick?.invoke()
        }
        
        btnRecord.setOnClickListener {
            onRecordClick?.invoke()
        }
        
        btnClose.setOnClickListener {
            onDismiss?.invoke()
        }
        
        // Make the whole view draggable
        setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            handleTouch(event)
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    private fun handleTouch(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        when (event.action) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                dX = x - event.rawX
                dY = y - event.rawY
                lastX = event.rawX
                lastY = event.rawY
                isDragging = false
                return true
            }
            
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                val deltaX = event.rawX - lastX
                val deltaY = event.rawY - lastY
                
                if (Math.abs(deltaX) > touchSlop || Math.abs(deltaY) > touchSlop) {
                    isDragging = true
                }
                
                if (isDragging) {
                    // Move the view
                    val newX = event.rawX + dX
                    val newY = event.rawY + dY
                    
                    // Clamp to screen bounds
                    val parent = parent as? View
                    parent?.let {
                        val maxX = it.width - width
                        val maxY = it.height - height
                        x = newX.coerceIn(0f, maxX.toFloat())
                        y = newY.coerceIn(0f, maxY.toFloat())
                    }
                    
                    lastX = event.rawX
                    lastY = event.rawY
                }
                return true
            }
            
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                if (!isDragging) {
                    // It was a tap, not a drag. Let children handle it.
                    // We already have click listeners, so we return false here.
                    performClick()
                }
                isDragging = false
                return true
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
        super.performClick()
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Set the state of the record button (Recording / Not Recording).
     */
    fun setRecordingState(isRecording: Boolean) {
        if (isRecording) {
            btnRecord.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_stop)
            btnRecord.setColorFilter(context.getColor(R.color.danger_red))
        } else {
            btnRecord.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_record)
            btnRecord.setColorFilter(context.getColor(R.color.primary_cyan))
        }
    }
}

Overlay 4: Custom aiming crosshair that can be dragged and repositioned.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.fspedx.booster.R

/**
 * Aiming crosshair overlay that can be dragged anywhere on the screen.
 * Useful for aiming passes or shots in PES.
 * Renders a neon green/grey crosshair with a central dot.
 */
class CrosshairOverlayView(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null
) : View(context, attrs) {

    companion object {
        private const val CROSSHAIR_SIZE_DP = 40f
        private const val GAP_SIZE_DP = 8f
        private const val DOT_RADIUS_DP = 4f
        private const val LINE_WIDTH_DP = 2f
    }

    // Paint objects
    private val paint = Paint().apply {
        isAntiAlias = true
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = dpToPx(LINE_WIDTH_DP)
        color = ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.primary_cyan)
    }

    private val dotPaint = Paint().apply {
        isAntiAlias = true
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.primary_cyan)
    }

    private val shadowPaint = Paint().apply {
        isAntiAlias = true
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = dpToPx(LINE_WIDTH_DP)
        color = Color.BLACK
        alpha = 100
    }

    // Dimensions
    private var crosshairSize = dpToPx(CROSSHAIR_SIZE_DP)
    private var gapSize = dpToPx(GAP_SIZE_DP)
    private var dotRadius = dpToPx(DOT_RADIUS_DP)
    
    // Drag state
    private var dX = 0f
    private var dY = 0f
    private var isDragging = false
    private val touchSlop = 10f

    init {
        setLayerType(LAYER_TYPE_SOFTWARE, null) // For transparency
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        
        val cx = width / 2f
        val cy = height / 2f
        
        // Draw shadow (offset slightly for depth)
        canvas.save()
        canvas.translate(2f, 2f)
        drawCrosshair(canvas, cx, cy, shadowPaint)
        canvas.restore()
        
        // Draw main crosshair
        drawCrosshair(canvas, cx, cy, paint)
        
        // Draw center dot
        canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, dotRadius, dotPaint)
    }

    private fun drawCrosshair(canvas: Canvas, cx: Float, cy: Float, paint: Paint) {
        // Top line
        canvas.drawLine(cx, cy - gapSize, cx, cy - gapSize - crosshairSize, paint)
        // Bottom line
        canvas.drawLine(cx, cy + gapSize, cx, cy + gapSize + crosshairSize, paint)
        // Left line
        canvas.drawLine(cx - gapSize, cy, cx - gapSize - crosshairSize, cy, paint)
        // Right line
        canvas.drawLine(cx + gapSize, cy, cx + gapSize + crosshairSize, cy, paint)
    }

    @SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        when (event.action) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                dX = x - event.rawX
                dY = y - event.rawY
                isDragging = false
                return true
            }
            
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                val deltaX = event.rawX - (x + dX)
                val deltaY = event.rawY - (y + dY)
                
                if (Math.abs(deltaX) > touchSlop || Math.abs(deltaY) > touchSlop) {
                    isDragging = true
                }
                
                if (isDragging) {
                    val newX = event.rawX + dX
                    val newY = event.rawY + dY
                    
                    val parent = parent as? View
                    parent?.let {
                        val maxX = it.width - width
                        val maxY = it.height - height
                        x = newX.coerceIn(0f, maxX.toFloat())
                        y = newY.coerceIn(0f, maxY.toFloat())
                    }
                    invalidate()
                }
                return true
            }
            
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                if (!isDragging) {
                    performClick()
                }
                isDragging = false
                return true
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
        super.performClick()
        return true
    }

    private fun dpToPx(dp: Float): Float {
        return dp * resources.displayMetrics.density
    }
}

package com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews

import android.animation.ValueAnimator
import android.content.Context
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.ProgressBar
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.constraintlayout.widget.ConstraintLayout
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants

/**
 * Side panel that slides out from the left edge.
 * Displays live performance and network stats.
 * Users can swipe to open/close.
 */
class SidePanelDrawerView(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null
) : ConstraintLayout(context, attrs) {

    // UI Elements
    private lateinit var tvFps: TextView
    private lateinit var tvTemp: TextView
    private lateinit var tvBattery: TextView
    private lateinit var tvPing: TextView
    private lateinit var tvPacketLoss: TextView
    private lateinit var tvNetworkMode: TextView
    private lateinit var tvRam: TextView
    private lateinit var progressRam: ProgressBar
    
    // Toggle switches (using custom views or standard switches)
    private lateinit var toggleRamBooster: View
    private lateinit var toggleDnd: View
    private lateinit var toggleVpn: View
    private lateinit var toggleThermal: View

    // Panel state
    private var isOpen = false
    private val animator = ValueAnimator()
    
    // Constants
    private val panelWidthPx = (context.resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels * 0.75).toInt()

    init {
        // Inflate the panel layout
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(context).inflate(R.layout.view_side_panel, this, true)
        
        // Bind UI elements
        tvFps = findViewById(R.id.tvStatFps)
        tvTemp = findViewById(R.id.tvStatTemp)
        tvBattery = findViewById(R.id.tvStatBattery)
        tvPing = findViewById(R.id.tvStatPing)
        tvPacketLoss = findViewById(R.id.tvStatPacketLoss)
        tvNetworkMode = findViewById(R.id.tvStatNetwork)
        tvRam = findViewById(R.id.tvStatRam)
        progressRam = findViewById(R.id.progressStatRam)
        
        // Toggle views (these are custom layouts that act as switches)
        toggleRamBooster = findViewById(R.id.toggleRamBooster)
        toggleDnd = findViewById(R.id.toggleDnd)
        toggleVpn = findViewById(R.id.toggleVpn)
        toggleThermal = findViewById(R.id.toggleThermal)
        
        // Set initial state (collapsed off-screen)
        translationX = -panelWidthPx.toFloat()
        visibility = View.GONE
        
        // Touch handling for swipe detection is managed by OverlayService
        // This view just renders and handles internal click events
    }

    /**
     * Open the panel with animation.
     */
    fun openPanel() {
        if (isOpen) return
        visibility = View.VISIBLE
        animate()
            .translationX(0f)
            .setDuration(300)
            .start()
        isOpen = true
        updateStats()
    }

    /**
     * Close the panel with animation.
     */
    fun closePanel() {
        if (!isOpen) return
        animate()
            .translationX(-panelWidthPx.toFloat())
            .setDuration(300)
            .withEndAction {
                visibility = View.GONE
            }
            .start()
        isOpen = false
    }

    /**
     * Toggle the panel open/closed.
     */
    fun togglePanel() {
        if (isOpen) {
            closePanel()
        } else {
            openPanel()
        }
    }

    fun isOpen(): Boolean = isOpen

    /**
     * Update all stats displayed in the panel.
     * Called every second by OverlayService.
     */
    fun updateStats() {
        // These values should be pulled from ViewModels or SharedPreferences
        // For now, placeholder values:
        tvFps.text = "58 FPS"
        tvTemp.text = "41°C"
        tvBattery.text = "67%"
        tvPing.text = "42 ms"
        tvPacketLoss.text = "0.3%"
        tvNetworkMode.text = "Wi-Fi (Low-Latency)"
        
        // RAM stats
        val usedMB = 2800
        val totalMB = 4096
        tvRam.text = "$usedMB / $totalMB MB"
        progressRam.max = totalMB
        progressRam.progress = usedMB
        
        // Update toggle states (visual only - actual state comes from prefs)
        // Toggle views can be checked/unchecked
    }

    /**
     * Set the state of a toggle in the panel.
     * This should reflect the actual feature state.
     */
    fun setToggleState(toggleId: Int, isChecked: Boolean) {
        when (toggleId) {
            R.id.toggleRamBooster -> {
                toggleRamBooster.isSelected = isChecked
                toggleRamBooster.setBackgroundResource(
                    if (isChecked) R.drawable.bg_toggle_on else R.drawable.bg_toggle_off
                )
            }
            R.id.toggleDnd -> {
                toggleDnd.isSelected = isChecked
                toggleDnd.setBackgroundResource(
                    if (isChecked) R.drawable.bg_toggle_on else R.drawable.bg_toggle_off
                )
            }
            R.id.toggleVpn -> {
                toggleVpn.isSelected = isChecked
                toggleVpn.setBackgroundResource(
                    if (isChecked) R.drawable.bg_toggle_on else R.drawable.bg_toggle_off
                )
            }
            R.id.toggleThermal -> {
                toggleThermal.isSelected = isChecked
                toggleThermal.setBackgroundResource(
                    if (isChecked) R.drawable.bg_toggle_on else R.drawable.bg_toggle_off
                )
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Simulate a swipe gesture from the edge.
     * Called by OverlayService when a left-edge swipe is detected.
     */
    fun onSwipeDetected() {
        togglePanel()
    }
}

Core: Local VPN tunnel with UDP priority and DNS hijacking.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.services

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.VpnService
import android.os.Build
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.activities.MainActivity
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.SocketAddress

/**
 * VPN Service that routes only PES traffic through a local tunnel.
 * Prioritizes UDP packets for real-time gameplay.
 */
class GameVpnService : VpnService() {

    private var vpnInterface: ParcelFileDescriptor? = null
    private var isRunning = false
    private var packetThread: Thread? = null

    companion object {
        private var instance: GameVpnService? = null

        fun start(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, GameVpnService::class.java)
            context.startService(intent)
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, GameVpnService::class.java)
            context.stopService(intent)
        }

        fun isRunning(): Boolean = instance != null && instance?.isRunning == true
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        startVpn()
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startVpn() {
        if (isRunning) return

        try {
            val builder = Builder()
            builder.setSession("FSPEDX Network Tunnel")
            builder.addAddress(Constants.VPN_ADDRESS, Constants.VPN_PREFIX_LENGTH)
            
            // Smart DNS - Cloudflare
            builder.addDnsServer(Constants.DNS_CLOUDFLARE)
            builder.addDnsServer(Constants.DNS_CLOUDFLARE_ALT)
            
            // Only route PES traffic through the VPN
            val prefs = (application as FSPEDXApplication).prefs
            val useSmartDns = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_SMART_DNS, true)
            val useLowLatency = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_WIFI_LOW_LATENCY, true)
            
            // Add allowed application (PES)
            builder.addAllowedApplication(Constants.PES_PACKAGE_NAME)
            builder.addAllowedApplication(Constants.PES_PACKAGE_NAME_ALT)
            
            // Block all other apps from using this VPN
            builder.setBlocking(true)

            // Establish the VPN interface
            vpnInterface = builder.establish()
            isRunning = true

            // Show foreground notification
            startForeground(Constants.NOTIFICATION_ID_VPN, buildNotification())

            // Start packet processing
            startPacketProcessing()

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            isRunning = false
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun startPacketProcessing() {
        val fd = vpnInterface?.fileDescriptor ?: return
        
        packetThread = Thread {
            val inputStream = FileInputStream(fd)
            val outputStream = FileOutputStream(fd)
            val buffer = ByteArray(32767)

            while (isRunning) {
                try {
                    val length = inputStream.read(buffer)
                    if (length > 0) {
                        val packet = buffer.copyOf(length)
                        processPacket(packet, outputStream)
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Socket closed or error
                    break
                }
            }
        }
        packetThread?.start()
    }

    /**
     * Process packets: prioritize UDP over TCP.
     * UDP = real-time game actions (passes, shots, movement).
     * TCP = non-critical data.
     */
    private fun processPacket(packet: ByteArray, outputStream: FileOutputStream) {
        try {
            // IP header: protocol is at byte 9
            // 17 = UDP, 6 = TCP
            val protocol = packet[9].toInt() and 0xFF
            
            // UDP gets immediate priority
            if (protocol == 17) {
                // Prioritize UDP - write immediately
                outputStream.write(packet)
                outputStream.flush()
            } else {
                // TCP - slight delay to prioritize UDP (or just write normally)
                // For simplicity, we just write it
                outputStream.write(packet)
                outputStream.flush()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Silently skip corrupted packets
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        isRunning = false
        packetThread?.interrupt()
        packetThread = null
        try {
            vpnInterface?.close()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore
        }
        vpnInterface = null
        instance = null
        stopForeground(true)
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java)
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0, intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val stopIntent = Intent(this, GameVpnService::class.java)
        stopIntent.action = Constants.ACTION_STOP_VPN
        val stopPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 1, stopIntent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_VPN)
            .setContentTitle("FSPEDX VPN Active")
            .setContentText("Traffic optimized for PES/eFootball")
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_vpn_lock)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .addAction(R.drawable.ic_stop, "Stop VPN", stopPendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .build()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_VPN,
                "FSPEDX VPN Tunnel",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Shows when the VPN tunnel is active"
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            manager?.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }
}
File 17: java/com/fspedx/booster/services/OverlayService.kt
Hosts all overlays: FPS counter, Floating Pill, Crosshair, and Side Panel.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.services

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.PixelFormat
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.activities.MainActivity
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews.CrosshairOverlayView
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews.FloatingPillView
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.customviews.SidePanelDrawerView
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.ThermalUtils

/**
 * Service that manages all system overlays:
 * - FPS / Temperature counter (top-right corner)
 * - Floating Quick-Pill (draggable, right edge)
 * - Aiming Crosshair (draggable, anywhere)
 * - Side Pull-Out Table (swipe from left)
 */
class OverlayService : android.app.Service() {

    private lateinit var windowManager: WindowManager
    private var isRunning = false
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    // Overlay views
    private var fpsView: View? = null
    private var floatingPill: FloatingPillView? = null
    private var crosshair: CrosshairOverlayView? = null
    private var sidePanel: SidePanelDrawerView? = null

    // FPS counter
    private var frameCount = 0
    private var lastFpsUpdate = 0L
    private var currentFps = 0

    companion object {
        private var instance: OverlayService? = null

        fun start(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java)
            context.startService(intent)
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java)
            context.stopService(intent)
        }

        fun isRunning(): Boolean = instance != null

        fun showFloatingPill(context: Context) {
            instance?.showPill(true)
        }

        fun hideFloatingPill(context: Context) {
            instance?.showPill(false)
        }

        fun showCrosshair(context: Context) {
            instance?.showCrosshair(true)
        }

        fun hideCrosshair(context: Context) {
            instance?.showCrosshair(false)
        }

        fun toggleSidePanel() {
            instance?.sidePanel?.togglePanel()
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        windowManager = getSystemService(WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
        createNotificationChannel()
        isRunning = true
        startForeground(Constants.NOTIFICATION_ID_OVERLAY, buildNotification())
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        // Handle incoming actions
        intent?.action?.let { action ->
            when (action) {
                Constants.ACTION_TOGGLE_PILL -> {
                    floatingPill?.let {
                        if (it.visibility == View.VISIBLE) {
                            it.visibility = View.GONE
                        } else {
                            it.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Start overlays if not already running
        if (fpsView == null) {
            setupFpsOverlay()
        }
        
        val prefs = (application as FSPEDXApplication).prefs
        if (prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_FLOATING_PILL, true)) {
            setupFloatingPill()
        }
        if (prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_CROSSHAIR, false)) {
            setupCrosshair()
        }
        if (prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_SIDE_PANEL, true)) {
            setupSidePanel()
        }

        startUpdatingStats()

        return START_STICKY
    }

    // ============ FPS OVERLAY ============

    private fun setupFpsOverlay() {
        if (fpsView != null) return
        if (!(application as FSPEDXApplication).prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_FPS_OVERLAY, true)) return

        val inflater = getSystemService(LAYOUT_INFLATER_SERVICE) as LayoutInflater
        fpsView = inflater.inflate(R.layout.view_fps_overlay, null)
        val tvFps = fpsView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvFps)
        val tvTemp = fpsView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTemp)

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY
            else
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_WATCH_OUTSIDE_TOUCH,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.END
            x = 16
            y = 80
        }

        windowManager.addView(fpsView, params)
    }

    // ============ FLOATING PILL ============

    private fun setupFloatingPill() {
        if (floatingPill != null) return

        floatingPill = FloatingPillView(this).apply {
            setOnBoostClick {
                // Trigger RAM boost
                android.widget.Toast.makeText(
                    this@OverlayService,
                    "RAM Boost triggered!",
                    android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
                ).show()
                // TODO: Call RamCleaner.forceClean()
            }
            setOnRecordClick {
                // Toggle recording
                if (SessionRecorderService.isRunning()) {
                    SessionRecorderService.stop(this@OverlayService)
                    setRecordingState(false)
                } else {
                    SessionRecorderService.start(this@OverlayService)
                    setRecordingState(true)
                }
            }
            setOnDismiss {
                floatingPill?.visibility = View.GONE
            }
        }

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY
            else
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.END
            x = 16
            y = 160
        }

        windowManager.addView(floatingPill, params)
    }

    private fun showPill(show: Boolean) {
        floatingPill?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
    }

    // ============ CROSSHAIR ============

    private fun setupCrosshair() {
        if (crosshair != null) return

        crosshair = CrosshairOverlayView(this)

        val size = (80 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            size,
            size,
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY
            else
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            x = 0
            y = 0
        }

        windowManager.addView(crosshair, params)
    }

    private fun showCrosshair(show: Boolean) {
        crosshair?.visibility = if (show) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
    }

    // ============ SIDE PANEL ============

    private fun setupSidePanel() {
        if (sidePanel != null) return

        sidePanel = SidePanelDrawerView(this)

        val panelWidth = (resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels * 0.75).toInt()
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            panelWidth,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY
            else
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.START or Gravity.TOP
            x = -panelWidth // Hidden off-screen initially
            y = 0
        }

        windowManager.addView(sidePanel, params)
        // Start closed
        sidePanel?.closePanel()
    }

    // ============ STATS UPDATES ============

    private fun startUpdatingStats() {
        val runnable = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                updateStats()
                if (isRunning) {
                    mainHandler.postDelayed(this, Constants.OVERLAY_UPDATE_INTERVAL_MS)
                }
            }
        }
        mainHandler.post(runnable)
    }

    private fun updateStats() {
        // Update FPS counter
        val tvFps = fpsView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvFps)
        val tvTemp = fpsView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTemp)

        tvFps?.text = "$currentFps FPS"
        val temp = ThermalUtils.getBatteryTemperature(this)
        tvTemp?.text = "$temp°C"

        // Update side panel if open
        if (sidePanel?.isOpen() == true) {
            sidePanel?.updateStats()
        }
    }

    // Called from Choreographer via a callback in the future
    fun onFrame() {
        frameCount++
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (now - lastFpsUpdate >= 1000) {
            currentFps = frameCount
            frameCount = 0
            lastFpsUpdate = now
        }
    }

    // ============ OVERRIDES ============

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        isRunning = false
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)

        // Remove all views
        try {
            fpsView?.let { windowManager.removeView(it) }
            floatingPill?.let { windowManager.removeView(it) }
            crosshair?.let { windowManager.removeView(it) }
            sidePanel?.let { windowManager.removeView(it) }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // View already removed
        }

        fpsView = null
        floatingPill = null
        crosshair = null
        sidePanel = null
        instance = null
        stopForeground(true)
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    // ============ NOTIFICATIONS ============

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java)
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0, intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_OVERLAY)
            .setContentTitle("FSPEDX Active")
            .setContentText("Optimizing PES performance")
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_speed)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .build()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_OVERLAY,
                "FSPEDX Overlay",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Shows when FSPEDX overlays are active"
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            manager?.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }
}
File 18: java/com/fspedx/booster/services/TouchAccessibilityService.kt
Captures touch events for heatmap generation and gesture classification.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.services

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService
import android.accessibilityservice.GestureDescription
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo
import com.fspedx.booster.data.database.AppDatabase
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.TouchEvent
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import kotlinx.coroutines.*

/**
 * Accessibility Service that records all touch events.
 * Classifies touches as Tap, Swipe, or Hold.
 * Data is saved to Room database for heatmap generation.
 */
class TouchAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {

    private var isRecording = false
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO + SupervisorJob())

    // Gesture tracking
    private var touchStartX = 0f
    private var touchStartY = 0f
    private var touchStartTime = 0L
    private var isTouchDown = false

    companion object {
        private var instance: TouchAccessibilityService? = null

        fun isRunning(): Boolean = instance != null

        fun toggleRecording(context: Context) {
            instance?.isRecording = !instance?.isRecording ?: false
        }

        fun startRecording() {
            instance?.isRecording = true
        }

        fun stopRecording() {
            instance?.isRecording = false
        }
    }

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected()
        instance = this
        isRecording = true
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        if (!isRecording) return
        if (event == null) return

        when (event.eventType) {
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_VIEW_CLICKED -> {
                // Tap detected (short click)
                val node = event.source
                node?.let {
                    val rect = android.graphics.Rect()
                    it.getBoundsInScreen(rect)
                    val x = rect.centerX().toFloat()
                    val y = rect.centerY().toFloat()
                    saveTouchEvent(x, y, TouchEvent.TYPE_TAP)
                }
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_TOUCH_INTERACTION_START -> {
                // Touch down
                val x = event.x.toFloatOrNull() ?: 0f
                val y = event.y.toFloatOrNull() ?: 0f
                touchStartX = x
                touchStartY = y
                touchStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                isTouchDown = true
            }
            AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_TOUCH_INTERACTION_END -> {
                // Touch up
                if (isTouchDown) {
                    val duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - touchStartTime
                    val x = event.x.toFloatOrNull() ?: touchStartX
                    val y = event.y.toFloatOrNull() ?: touchStartY
                    
                    val type = when {
                        duration > 500 -> TouchEvent.TYPE_HOLD
                        duration > 100 -> TouchEvent.TYPE_TAP
                        else -> TouchEvent.TYPE_TAP // Short tap
                    }
                    
                    // Check if it moved (swipe)
                    val deltaX = x - touchStartX
                    val deltaY = y - touchStartY
                    val distance = kotlin.math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaY * deltaY)
                    
                    val finalType = if (distance > 100) TouchEvent.TYPE_SWIPE else type
                    
                    saveTouchEvent(x, y, finalType)
                    isTouchDown = false
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun saveTouchEvent(x: Float, y: Float, type: String) {
        serviceScope.launch {
            try {
                val db = AppDatabase.getInstance(applicationContext)
                val touchEvent = TouchEvent(
                    x = x,
                    y = y,
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    type = type
                )
                db.touchEventDao().insert(touchEvent)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onGesture(gesture: GestureDescription?): Boolean {
        // Handle complex gestures if needed
        return super.onGesture(gesture)
    }

    override fun onInterrupt() {
        // Service interrupted
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        instance = null
        serviceScope.cancel()
    }
}
File 19: java/com/fspedx/booster/services/NotificationListenerService.kt
Smart DND: Mutes calls and notifications when PES is in the foreground.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.services

import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.service.notification.NotificationListenerService
import android.service.notification.StatusBarNotification
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants

/**
 * Listens for incoming notifications and mutes them when PES is active.
 * Also handles call blocking via DND mode.
 */
class NotificationListenerService : NotificationListenerService() {

    private var isDndEnabled = false
    private var originalRingerMode = AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_NORMAL

    companion object {
        private var instance: NotificationListenerService? = null

        fun isRunning(): Boolean = instance != null

        fun toggleDnd(context: Context) {
            instance?.toggleDndMode()
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        
        // Check if DND is enabled in settings
        val prefs = (application as FSPEDXApplication).prefs
        isDndEnabled = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_DND_MODE, true)
        
        if (isDndEnabled) {
            enableDnd()
        }
    }

    override fun onNotificationPosted(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) {
        if (!isDndEnabled) return
        if (sbn == null) return

        // Check if PES is in the foreground
        val app = application as FSPEDXApplication
        if (!app.isPesForeground.value) return

        // Cancel the notification to prevent sound/vibration
        try {
            val notificationManager = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            notificationManager.cancel(sbn.packageName, sbn.id)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore
        }

        // Also mute audio if ringer is on
        val audioManager = getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
        if (audioManager.ringerMode == AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_NORMAL) {
            audioManager.ringerMode = AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_SILENT
        }
    }

    override fun onNotificationRemoved(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) {
        // Restore ringer if no notifications are active and PES is not running
        val app = application as FSPEDXApplication
        if (!app.isPesForeground.value) {
            restoreRinger()
        }
    }

    override fun onListenerConnected() {
        super.onListenerConnected()
        instance = this
    }

    override fun onListenerDisconnected() {
        super.onListenerDisconnected()
        instance = null
        restoreRinger()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        instance = null
        restoreRinger()
    }

    private fun enableDnd() {
        val audioManager = getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
        originalRingerMode = audioManager.ringerMode
        
        // Set to silent or vibrate
        if (audioManager.ringerMode == AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_NORMAL) {
            audioManager.ringerMode = AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_SILENT
        }
    }

    private fun restoreRinger() {
        val audioManager = getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager
        if (audioManager.ringerMode != AudioManager.RINGER_MODE_NORMAL) {
            audioManager.ringerMode = originalRingerMode
        }
    }

    private fun toggleDndMode() {
        isDndEnabled = !isDndEnabled
        
        val prefs = (application as FSPEDXApplication).prefs
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_DND_MODE, isDndEnabled).apply()
        
        if (isDndEnabled) {
            enableDnd()
        } else {
            restoreRinger()
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the notification is from a phone call.
     * Calls are always high-priority and should be handled separately.
     */
    private fun isCallNotification(sbn: StatusBarNotification): Boolean {
        val packageName = sbn.packageName
        return packageName == "com.android.dialer" ||
                packageName == "com.android.incallui" ||
                packageName == "com.google.android.dialer"
    }
}
