Handles screen recording with heatmap overlay for session replays.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.services

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.hardware.display.DisplayManager
import android.hardware.display.VirtualDisplay
import android.media.MediaRecorder
import android.media.projection.MediaProjection
import android.media.projection.MediaProjectionManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Environment
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import android.provider.MediaStore
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.fspedx.booster.R
import com.fspedx.booster.ui.activities.MainActivity
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

/**
 * Service that records the screen with a heatmap overlay.
 * Saves videos to /Movies/FSPEDX/Recordings/
 */
class SessionRecorderService : android.app.Service() {

    private var mediaProjection: MediaProjection? = null
    private var virtualDisplay: VirtualDisplay? = null
    private var mediaRecorder: MediaRecorder? = null
    private var isRecording = false
    private var resultCode = 0
    private var data: Intent? = null

    companion object {
        private var instance: SessionRecorderService? = null

        fun start(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, SessionRecorderService::class.java)
            context.startService(intent)
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, SessionRecorderService::class.java)
            context.stopService(intent)
        }

        fun isRunning(): Boolean = instance != null && instance?.isRecording == true

        fun setMediaProjection(resultCode: Int, data: Intent) {
            instance?.resultCode = resultCode
            instance?.data = data
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        // Start recording when service starts
        if (!isRecording) {
            startRecording()
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startRecording() {
        if (isRecording) return
        if (mediaProjection == null) {
            // Need to get MediaProjection from the activity via callback
            // This is a placeholder - actual implementation requires activity result
            // We'll handle it via setMediaProjection()
            return
        }

        try {
            val outputFile = createOutputFile()
            mediaRecorder = MediaRecorder().apply {
                setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
                setVideoSource(MediaRecorder.VideoSource.SURFACE)
                setOutputFormat(MediaRecorder.OutputFormat.MPEG_4)
                setAudioEncoder(MediaRecorder.AudioEncoder.AAC)
                setVideoEncoder(MediaRecorder.VideoEncoder.H264)
                setVideoEncodingBitRate(4_000_000)
                setVideoFrameRate(30)
                setVideoSize(1080, 1920)
                setOutputFile(outputFile.absolutePath)
                prepare()
            }

            virtualDisplay = mediaProjection?.createVirtualDisplay(
                "FSPEDX Recording",
                1080,
                1920,
                resources.displayMetrics.densityDpi,
                DisplayManager.VIRTUAL_DISPLAY_FLAG_AUTO_MIRROR,
                mediaRecorder?.surface,
                null,
                null
            )

            mediaRecorder?.start()
            isRecording = true
            startForeground(Constants.NOTIFICATION_ID_RECORDING, buildNotification())

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            stopRecording()
        }
    }

    private fun stopRecording() {
        try {
            mediaRecorder?.stop()
            mediaRecorder?.release()
            mediaRecorder = null

            virtualDisplay?.release()
            virtualDisplay = null

            mediaProjection?.stop()
            mediaProjection = null

            isRecording = false
            stopForeground(true)

            android.widget.Toast.makeText(
                this,
                "Recording saved to Downloads",
                android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
            ).show()

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
        stopSelf()
    }

    private fun createOutputFile(): File {
        val recordingsDir = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES)
            ?.resolve(Constants.RECORDINGS_DIR)
        
        recordingsDir?.mkdirs()

        val timestamp = SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
        val fileName = "FSPEDX_$timestamp.mp4"

        return File(recordingsDir, fileName)
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java)
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0, intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val stopIntent = Intent(this, SessionRecorderService::class.java)
        stopIntent.action = Constants.ACTION_STOP_RECORDING
        val stopPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 1, stopIntent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_RECORDING)
            .setContentTitle("Recording Session")
            .setContentText("Recording gameplay with heatmap overlay...")
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_record)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .addAction(R.drawable.ic_stop, "Stop", stopPendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .build()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                Constants.NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_RECORDING,
                "FSPEDX Recording",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Shows when a session is being recorded"
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            manager?.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        instance = null
        if (isRecording) {
            stopRecording()
        }
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
}

Manages HomeFragment state: Ping, RAM usage, Boost status, and last ping timestamp.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.viewmodels

import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.data.repository.StatsRepository
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.network.NetworkUtils
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.RamCleaner
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.ThermalUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class MainViewModel(
    private val repository: StatsRepository = StatsRepository()
) : ViewModel() {

    // ============ LIVE DATA ============

    // Ping
    private val _lastPing = MutableLiveData<Int>()
    val lastPing: LiveData<Int> = _lastPing

    // RAM Usage (Pair: Used MB, Total MB)
    private val _ramUsage = MutableLiveData<Pair<Int, Int>>()
    val ramUsage: LiveData<Pair<Int, Int>> = _ramUsage

    // Boost Status (0 = idle, 1 = boosting, 2 = complete)
    private val _boostStatus = MutableLiveData(0)
    val boostStatus: LiveData<Int> = _boostStatus

    // Last boost result (MB freed)
    private val _lastBoostResult = MutableLiveData(0)
    val lastBoostResult: LiveData<Int> = _lastBoostResult

    // Battery and Temperature (combined)
    private val _batteryInfo = MutableLiveData<Pair<Int, Double>>() // (Level %, Temp °C)
    val batteryInfo: LiveData<Pair<Int, Double>> = _batteryInfo

    // ============ INIT ============

    init {
        // Load initial data
        loadPingFromPrefs()
        updateRamUsage()
        updateBatteryInfo()
    }

    // ============ PUBLIC METHODS ============

    /**
     * Perform a RAM boost (80% priority lock).
     * Runs in background, updates UI via LiveData.
     */
    fun performBoost(whitelist: Set<String>) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _boostStatus.value = 1 // Boosting

            val freedMB = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                RamCleaner.forceClean(
                    FSPEDXApplication.instance,
                    whitelist
                )
            }

            _lastBoostResult.value = freedMB
            _boostStatus.value = 2 // Complete

            // Update RAM usage after boost
            updateRamUsage()

            // Reset status after 3 seconds
            delay(3000)
            _boostStatus.value = 0 // Idle
        }
    }

    /**
     * Run a manual ping test to the default host.
     */
    fun runPingTest() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val ping = NetworkUtils.ping(Constants.PING_DEFAULT_HOST)
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                if (ping > 0) {
                    _lastPing.value = ping
                    // Save to SharedPreferences
                    val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
                    prefs.edit().apply {
                        putInt(Constants.KEY_LAST_PING, ping)
                        putLong(Constants.KEY_LAST_PING_TIMESTAMP, System.currentTimeMillis())
                        apply()
                    }
                } else {
                    // If ping fails, keep the last known value
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Update RAM usage from the system.
     */
    fun updateRamUsage() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val usage = RamCleaner.getRamUsage(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                _ramUsage.value = usage
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Update battery level and temperature.
     */
    fun updateBatteryInfo() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val level = ThermalUtils.getBatteryLevel(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            val temp = ThermalUtils.getBatteryTemperature(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                _batteryInfo.value = Pair(level, temp)
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ PRIVATE HELPERS ============

    private fun loadPingFromPrefs() {
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        val ping = prefs.getInt(Constants.KEY_LAST_PING, 0)
        if (ping > 0) {
            _lastPing.value = ping
        }
    }

    // ============ CLEANUP ============

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        // Cancel any ongoing tasks if needed
    }
}

Manages network-related state: VPN status, DNS, Dual-Channel, and connection mode.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.viewmodels

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.Network
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.net.NetworkRequest
import android.os.Build
import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.services.GameVpnService
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class NetworkViewModel : ViewModel() {

    // ============ STATE FLOWS (Modern) ============

    // VPN status
    private val _vpnStatus = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val vpnStatus: StateFlow<Boolean> = _vpnStatus.asStateFlow()

    // DNS status (true = using Smart DNS)
    private val _dnsStatus = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val dnsStatus: StateFlow<Boolean> = _dnsStatus.asStateFlow()

    // Dual-Channel status
    private val _dualChannelStatus = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val dualChannelStatus: StateFlow<Boolean> = _dualChannelStatus.asStateFlow()

    // Current network type
    private val _networkType = MutableStateFlow("Unknown")
    val networkType: StateFlow<String> = _networkType.asStateFlow()

    // Wi-Fi low-latency status
    private val _lowLatencyStatus = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val lowLatencyStatus: StateFlow<Boolean> = _lowLatencyStatus.asStateFlow()

    // Connection quality (0 = poor, 1 = fair, 2 = good, 3 = excellent)
    private val _connectionQuality = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val connectionQuality: StateFlow<Int> = _connectionQuality.asStateFlow()

    // ============ LIVE DATA (Legacy/Compat) ============

    private val _ping = MutableLiveData<Int>()
    val ping: LiveData<Int> = _ping

    private val _packetLoss = MutableLiveData<Float>()
    val packetLoss: LiveData<Float> = _packetLoss

    // ============ INIT ============

    init {
        // Load preferences
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        _vpnStatus.value = GameVpnService.isRunning()
        _dnsStatus.value = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_SMART_DNS, true)
        _dualChannelStatus.value = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_DUAL_CHANNEL, false)
        _lowLatencyStatus.value = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_WIFI_LOW_LATENCY, true)

        // Start network monitoring
        startNetworkMonitoring()
    }

    // ============ PUBLIC METHODS ============

    /**
     * Start monitoring network changes.
     * Updates networkType, connectionQuality, and ping automatically.
     */
    private fun startNetworkMonitoring() {
        val connectivityManager = FSPEDXApplication.instance.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager

        val networkCallback = object : ConnectivityManager.NetworkCallback() {
            override fun onAvailable(network: Network) {
                updateNetworkInfo()
            }

            override fun onLost(network: Network) {
                updateNetworkInfo()
            }

            override fun onCapabilitiesChanged(network: Network, networkCapabilities: NetworkCapabilities) {
                updateNetworkInfo()
            }
        }

        val request = NetworkRequest.Builder()
            .addCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET)
            .build()

        connectivityManager.registerNetworkCallback(request, networkCallback)

        // Initial update
        updateNetworkInfo()
    }

    /**
     * Update network info (type, quality).
     */
    private fun updateNetworkInfo() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val info = withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                getNetworkInfo(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            }
            _networkType.value = info.first
            _connectionQuality.value = info.second
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current network type and quality.
     * @return Pair(networkType, quality)
     * Quality: 0=poor, 1=fair, 2=good, 3=excellent
     */
    private fun getNetworkInfo(context: Context): Pair<String, Int> {
        val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
        val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return Pair("None", 0)
        val caps = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return Pair("None", 0)

        val type = when {
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) -> "Wi-Fi"
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) -> "Mobile Data"
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_ETHERNET) -> "Ethernet"
            else -> "Unknown"
        }

        // Estimate quality based on bandwidth and latency
        val downSpeed = caps.linkDownstreamBandwidthKbps
        val quality = when {
            downSpeed >= 100000 -> 3 // Excellent (>100 Mbps)
            downSpeed >= 20000 -> 2  // Good (>20 Mbps)
            downSpeed >= 5000 -> 1   // Fair (>5 Mbps)
            else -> 0                // Poor
        }

        return Pair(type, quality)
    }

    /**
     * Toggle Smart DNS.
     * If enabled, switches to Cloudflare DNS.
     */
    fun toggleSmartDNS(enabled: Boolean) {
        _dnsStatus.value = enabled
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_SMART_DNS, enabled).apply()

        // If VPN is running, restart it to apply new DNS
        if (GameVpnService.isRunning()) {
            GameVpnService.stop(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            // Give it a moment to stop, then restart
            android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                GameVpnService.start(FSPEDXApplication.instance)
            }, 500)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Toggle Dual-Channel Rescue.
     * Opens system settings for the user to enable.
     */
    fun toggleDualChannel(enabled: Boolean) {
        _dualChannelStatus.value = enabled
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_DUAL_CHANNEL, enabled).apply()
    }

    /**
     * Update VPN status.
     * Called when the VPN service starts or stops.
     */
    fun updateVpnStatus(isRunning: Boolean) {
        _vpnStatus.value = isRunning
    }

    /**
     * Update ping value from the network.
     */
    fun updatePing(pingMs: Int) {
        _ping.value = pingMs
        // Update packet loss estimate (placeholder)
        val loss = when {
            pingMs < 50 -> 0.0f
            pingMs < 100 -> 0.2f
            pingMs < 200 -> 1.0f
            else -> 2.5f
        }
        _packetLoss.value = loss
    }

    /**
     * Check if any network connection is available.
     */
    fun isNetworkAvailable(): Boolean {
        val connectivityManager = FSPEDXApplication.instance.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
        val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork
        val caps = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network)
        return caps != null && caps.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET)
    }
}
File 23: java/com/fspedx/booster/viewmodels/PerformanceViewModel.kt
Manages performance metrics: FPS, Temperature, Thermal Throttle, and Refresh Rate.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.viewmodels

import android.os.Build
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.RefreshRateUtils
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance.ThermalUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class PerformanceViewModel : ViewModel() {

    // ============ STATE FLOWS ============

    // FPS (frames per second)
    private val _fps = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val fps: StateFlow<Int> = _fps.asStateFlow()

    // Battery Temperature (°C)
    private val _temperature = MutableStateFlow(0.0)
    val temperature: StateFlow<Double> = _temperature.asStateFlow()

    // CPU Temperature (°C) - may not be available on all devices
    private val _cpuTemperature = MutableStateFlow(0.0)
    val cpuTemperature: StateFlow<Double> = _cpuTemperature.asStateFlow()

    // Battery Level (%)
    private val _batteryLevel = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val batteryLevel: StateFlow<Int> = _batteryLevel.asStateFlow()

    // Thermal Throttle Status (0=normal, 1=warning, 2=critical)
    private val _thermalStatus = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val thermalStatus: StateFlow<Int> = _thermalStatus.asStateFlow()

    // Current Refresh Rate (Hz)
    private val _refreshRate = MutableStateFlow(60)
    val refreshRate: StateFlow<Int> = _refreshRate.asStateFlow()

    // Is the refresh rate switch enabled (120Hz → 60Hz)
    private val _refreshRateSwitchEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val refreshRateSwitchEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _refreshRateSwitchEnabled.asStateFlow()

    // Is the thermal sentinel enabled
    private val _thermalSentinelEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val thermalSentinelEnabled: StateFlow<Boolean> = _thermalSentinelEnabled.asStateFlow()

    // ============ LIVE UPDATE LOOP ============

    private var updateJob: kotlinx.coroutines.Job? = null

    init {
        // Load preferences
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        _refreshRateSwitchEnabled.value = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_REFRESH_RATE_SWITCH, true)
        _thermalSentinelEnabled.value = prefs.getBoolean(Constants.KEY_THERMAL_SENTINEL, true)

        // Set initial refresh rate
        if (_refreshRateSwitchEnabled.value) {
            RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(FSPEDXApplication.instance, 60)
            _refreshRate.value = 60
        } else {
            _refreshRate.value = 120
        }

        // Start update loop
        startUpdateLoop()
    }

    private fun startUpdateLoop() {
        updateJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            while (true) {
                updateStats()
                delay(Constants.OVERLAY_UPDATE_INTERVAL_MS)
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun updateStats() {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val context = FSPEDXApplication.instance

            // Update temperature
            val temp = ThermalUtils.getBatteryTemperature(context)
            _temperature.value = temp

            // Update CPU temp (if available)
            val cpuTemp = ThermalUtils.getCpuTemperature()
            if (cpuTemp > 0) {
                _cpuTemperature.value = cpuTemp
            }

            // Update battery level
            val level = ThermalUtils.getBatteryLevel(context)
            _batteryLevel.value = level

            // Update thermal status
            _thermalStatus.value = when {
                temp >= Constants.TEMP_CRITICAL_THRESHOLD -> 2 // Critical
                temp >= Constants.TEMP_WARNING_THRESHOLD -> 1 // Warning
                else -> 0 // Normal
            }

            // Handle thermal sentinel auto-throttle
            if (_thermalSentinelEnabled.value && _thermalStatus.value >= 2) {
                // Critical temperature - force Eco mode? Or just notify.
                // We'll just update the status, and let the UI handle it.
                // The FeaturesFragment should display a warning.
            }

            // Update refresh rate if switch is enabled
            if (_refreshRateSwitchEnabled.value) {
                val currentRate = RefreshRateUtils.getRefreshRate(context)
                if (currentRate != 60) {
                    RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(context, 60)
                    _refreshRate.value = 60
                }
            } else {
                val currentRate = RefreshRateUtils.getRefreshRate(context)
                if (currentRate != 120) {
                    RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(context, 120)
                    _refreshRate.value = 120
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ PUBLIC METHODS ============

    /**
     * Update FPS from the OverlayService callback.
     * Called by the service via a broadcast or direct method.
     */
    fun updateFps(newFps: Int) {
        _fps.value = newFps
    }

    /**
     * Toggle the refresh rate switch (120Hz ↔ 60Hz).
     */
    fun toggleRefreshRateSwitch(enabled: Boolean) {
        _refreshRateSwitchEnabled.value = enabled
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_REFRESH_RATE_SWITCH, enabled).apply()

        val context = FSPEDXApplication.instance
        if (enabled) {
            RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(context, 60)
            _refreshRate.value = 60
        } else {
            RefreshRateUtils.setRefreshRate(context, 120)
            _refreshRate.value = 120
        }
    }

    /**
     * Toggle the thermal sentinel.
     */
    fun toggleThermalSentinel(enabled: Boolean) {
        _thermalSentinelEnabled.value = enabled
        val prefs = FSPEDXApplication.instance.prefs
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(Constants.KEY_THERMAL_SENTINEL, enabled).apply()
    }

    /**
     * Get a human-readable thermal status string.
     */
    fun getThermalStatusText(): String {
        return when (_thermalStatus.value) {
            0 -> "Normal ✅"
            1 -> "Warming ⚠️"
            2 -> "Critical 🔥"
            else -> "Unknown"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device needs cooling (temperature > warning).
     */
    fun needsCooling(): Boolean {
        return _thermalStatus.value >= 1
    }

    // ============ CLEANUP ============

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        updateJob?.cancel()
        updateJob = null
    }
}

Manages analytics data: Heatmap clusters, Mis-taps, and Lag Timeline charts.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.viewmodels

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.fspedx.booster.FSPEDXApplication
import com.fspedx.booster.data.database.AppDatabase
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.LagSpike
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.TouchEvent
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.ml.KMeansCluster
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class AnalyticsViewModel : ViewModel() {

    // ============ STATE FLOWS ============

    // All touch events (for heatmap)
    private val _touchEvents = MutableStateFlow<List<TouchEvent>>(emptyList())
    val touchEvents: StateFlow<List<TouchEvent>> = _touchEvents.asStateFlow()

    // Centroids from K-Means clustering
    private val _centroids = MutableStateFlow<List<Pair<Float, Float>>>(emptyList())
    val centroids: StateFlow<List<Pair<Float, Float>>> = _centroids.asStateFlow()

    // Mis-tap count (taps that missed the recommended zones)
    private val _misTapCount = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val misTapCount: StateFlow<Int> = _misTapCount.asStateFlow()

    // Mis-tap coordinates
    private val _misTapLocations = MutableStateFlow<List<Pair<Float, Float>>>(emptyList())
    val misTapLocations: StateFlow<List<Pair<Float, Float>>> = _misTapLocations.asStateFlow()

    // Lag Timeline data (for chart)
    private val _lagSpikes = MutableStateFlow<List<LagSpike>>(emptyList())
    val lagSpikes: StateFlow<List<LagSpike>> = _lagSpikes.asStateFlow()

    // Is K-Means processing
    private val _isProcessing = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isProcessing: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isProcessing.asStateFlow()

    // Current session ID for organizing recordings
    private var currentSessionId: Long = 0

    // ============ DATABASE REFERENCE ============

    private val database = AppDatabase.getInstance(FSPEDXApplication.instance)

    // ============ PUBLIC METHODS ============

    /**
     * Load all touch events from the database.
     */
    fun loadTouchEvents() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val events = database.touchEventDao().getAll()
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _touchEvents.value = events
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Load touch events for a specific session (by timestamp range).
     */
    fun loadTouchEventsForSession(startTime: Long, endTime: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val events = database.touchEventDao().getByTimestampRange(startTime, endTime)
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _touchEvents.value = events
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Run K-Means clustering on the loaded touch events.
     * @param k The number of clusters (buttons) to find.
     */
    fun findButtonCentroids(k: Int = 4) {
        if (_touchEvents.value.isEmpty()) {
            return
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            _isProcessing.value = true

            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                try {
                    // Convert TouchEvents to KMeansCluster.TouchPoint
                    val points = _touchEvents.value.map { event ->
                        KMeansCluster.TouchPoint(event.x, event.y)
                    }

                    // Run K-Means
                    val centroids = KMeansCluster.findCentroids(points, k)

                    withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                        _centroids.value = centroids.map { Pair(it.x, it.y) }
                        _isProcessing.value = false
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    e.printStackTrace()
                    withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                        _isProcessing.value = false
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Detect mis-taps based on the calculated centroids.
     * A mis-tap is a tap that is > 100px from any centroid.
     */
    fun detectMisTaps() {
        if (_centroids.value.isEmpty() || _touchEvents.value.isEmpty()) {
            return
        }

        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val centroidsList = _centroids.value.map { KMeansCluster.TouchPoint(it.first, it.second) }
                val events = _touchEvents.value
                val misTaps = mutableListOf<Pair<Float, Float>>()
                val threshold = 100f // 100px radius

                for (event in events) {
                    val point = KMeansCluster.TouchPoint(event.x, event.y)
                    val distances = centroidsList.map { centroid ->
                        kotlin.math.sqrt(
                            kotlin.math.pow(point.x - centroid.x, 2f) +
                            kotlin.math.pow(point.y - centroid.y, 2f)
                        )
                    }
                    val minDistance = distances.minOrNull() ?: Float.MAX_VALUE
                    if (minDistance > threshold) {
                        misTaps.add(Pair(event.x, event.y))
                    }
                }

                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _misTapCount.value = misTaps.size
                    _misTapLocations.value = misTaps
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Load lag spikes from the database for a specific session.
     */
    fun loadLagSpikes(sessionId: Long = currentSessionId) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val spikes = database.lagSpikeDao().getAll()
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _lagSpikes.value = spikes
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Clear all touch events from the database (start fresh).
     */
    fun clearTouchData() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                database.touchEventDao().deleteAll()
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _touchEvents.value = emptyList()
                    _centroids.value = emptyList()
                    _misTapCount.value = 0
                    _misTapLocations.value = emptyList()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Clear all lag spikes from the database.
     */
    fun clearLagSpikes() {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                database.lagSpikeDao().deleteAll()
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _lagSpikes.value = emptyList()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Export touch data as JSON for backup.
     */
    suspend fun exportTouchData(): String {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val events = _touchEvents.value
                val gson = com.google.gson.Gson()
                gson.toJson(events)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                "{}"
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Import touch data from JSON string.
     */
    suspend fun importTouchData(json: String) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val gson = com.google.gson.Gson()
                val events = gson.fromJson(json, Array<TouchEvent>::class.java).toList()
                database.touchEventDao().insertAll(events)
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _touchEvents.value = events
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ CLEANUP ============

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        // Close database connection if needed
    }
}

package com.fspedx.booster.data.models

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey

/**
 * Represents a single touch event captured by the Accessibility Service.
 * Used for heatmap generation and K-Means clustering.
 */
@Entity(tableName = "touch_events")
data class TouchEvent(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true)
    val id: Int = 0,
    val x: Float,                        // X coordinate on screen
    val y: Float,                        // Y coordinate on screen
    val timestamp: Long,                 // When the touch occurred (System.currentTimeMillis())
    val type: String = TYPE_TAP          // Tap, Swipe, or Hold
) {
    companion object {
        const val TYPE_TAP = "tap"
        const val TYPE_SWIPE = "swipe"
        const val TYPE_HOLD = "hold"
    }
}

package com.fspedx.booster.data.database

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import androidx.room.TypeConverters
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.LagSpike
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.TouchEvent
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants

/**
 * Room Database singleton.
 * Holds all tables: touch_events and lag_spikes.
 */
@Database(
    entities = [TouchEvent::class, LagSpike::class],
    version = Constants.DB_VERSION,
    exportSchema = false
)
@TypeConverters(Converters::class)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {

    abstract fun touchEventDao(): TouchEventDao
    abstract fun lagSpikeDao(): LagSpikeDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        fun getInstance(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    AppDatabase::class.java,
                    Constants.DB_NAME
                )
                    .fallbackToDestructiveMigration()
                    .build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }

        /**
         * For testing: clear the instance.
         */
        fun destroyInstance() {
            INSTANCE = null
        }
    }
}

package com.fspedx.booster.data.database

import androidx.room.TypeConverter
import java.util.Date

/**
 * Type converters for Room database.
 * Converts between Long and Date (if needed).
 */
class Converters {

    @TypeConverter
    fun fromTimestamp(value: Long?): Date? {
        return value?.let { Date(it) }
    }

    @TypeConverter
    fun dateToTimestamp(date: Date?): Long? {
        return date?.time
    }
}

CRUD operations for TouchEvent table.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.data.database

import androidx.room.*
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.TouchEvent
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

/**
 * Data Access Object for TouchEvent.
 * Provides insert, query, and delete methods.
 */
@Dao
interface TouchEventDao {

    /**
     * Insert a single touch event.
     */
    @Insert
    suspend fun insert(event: TouchEvent)

    /**
     * Insert multiple touch events.
     */
    @Insert
    suspend fun insertAll(events: List<TouchEvent>)

    /**
     * Get all touch events (ordered by timestamp).
     */
    @Query("SELECT * FROM touch_events ORDER BY timestamp ASC")
    fun getAll(): Flow<List<TouchEvent>>

    /**
     * Get touch events within a specific timestamp range.
     * Used for session-based filtering.
     */
    @Query("SELECT * FROM touch_events WHERE timestamp BETWEEN :startTime AND :endTime ORDER BY timestamp ASC")
    suspend fun getByTimestampRange(startTime: Long, endTime: Long): List<TouchEvent>

    /**
     * Get touch events of a specific type (Tap, Swipe, Hold).
     */
    @Query("SELECT * FROM touch_events WHERE type = :type ORDER BY timestamp ASC")
    suspend fun getByType(type: String): List<TouchEvent>

    /**
     * Count total number of touch events.
     */
    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM touch_events")
    suspend fun getCount(): Int

    /**
     * Get the most recent touch event.
     */
    @Query("SELECT * FROM touch_events ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getLatest(): TouchEvent?

    /**
     * Delete all touch events (reset heatmap data).
     */
    @Query("DELETE FROM touch_events")
    suspend fun deleteAll()

    /**
     * Delete touch events older than a certain timestamp.
     */
    @Query("DELETE FROM touch_events WHERE timestamp < :beforeTime")
    suspend fun deleteOlderThan(beforeTime: Long)
}

package com.fspedx.booster.data.repository

import android.content.Context
import com.fspedx.booster.data.database.AppDatabase
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.LagSpike
import com.fspedx.booster.data.models.TouchEvent
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.network.NetworkUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * Repository that bridges ViewModels with the data sources.
 * Handles both local (Room) and remote (Network) data.
 */
class StatsRepository(
    private val context: Context
) {

    // Lazy init the database
    private val database by lazy { AppDatabase.getInstance(context) }
    private val touchEventDao by lazy { database.touchEventDao() }
    private val lagSpikeDao by lazy { database.lagSpikeDao() }

    // ============ TOUCH EVENTS ============

    /**
     * Get all touch events as a Flow (reactive).
     */
    fun getAllTouchEvents(): Flow<List<TouchEvent>> {
        return touchEventDao.getAll()
    }

    /**
     * Get touch events by timestamp range (suspending).
     */
    suspend fun getTouchEventsByRange(startTime: Long, endTime: Long): List<TouchEvent> {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            touchEventDao.getByTimestampRange(startTime, endTime)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Insert a single touch event.
     */
    suspend fun insertTouchEvent(event: TouchEvent) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            touchEventDao.insert(event)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Insert multiple touch events.
     */
    suspend fun insertTouchEvents(events: List<TouchEvent>) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            touchEventDao.insertAll(events)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Delete all touch events.
     */
    suspend fun clearTouchEvents() {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            touchEventDao.deleteAll()
        }
    }

    /**
     * Count total touch events.
     */
    suspend fun getTouchEventCount(): Int {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            touchEventDao.getCount()
        }
    }

    // ============ LAG SPIKES ============

    /**
     * Get all lag spikes as a Flow (reactive).
     */
    fun getAllLagSpikes(): Flow<List<LagSpike>> {
        return lagSpikeDao.getAll()
    }

    /**
     * Get lag spikes by session ID.
     */
    suspend fun getLagSpikesBySession(sessionId: Long): List<LagSpike> {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            lagSpikeDao.getBySession(sessionId)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Insert a single lag spike.
     */
    suspend fun insertLagSpike(spike: LagSpike) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            lagSpikeDao.insert(spike)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Insert multiple lag spikes.
     */
    suspend fun insertLagSpikes(spikes: List<LagSpike>) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            lagSpikeDao.insertAll(spikes)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Delete all lag spikes.
     */
    suspend fun clearLagSpikes() {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            lagSpikeDao.deleteAll()
        }
    }

    // ============ NETWORK OPERATIONS ============

    /**
     * Perform a ping test to the default host.
     * @return Ping in milliseconds, or -1 if failed.
     */
    suspend fun runPingTest(host: String = "8.8.8.8"): Int {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            NetworkUtils.ping(host)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Perform a ping test and automatically save it as a LagSpike.
     * @return The ping value, or -1 if failed.
     */
    suspend fun runAndSavePingTest(
        sessionId: Long = 0,
        host: String = "8.8.8.8"
    ): Int {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val ping = NetworkUtils.ping(host)
            if (ping > 0) {
                val spike = LagSpike(
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    ping = ping,
                    sessionId = sessionId
                )
                lagSpikeDao.insert(spike)
            }
            ping
        }
    }

    /**
     * Start a continuous ping monitoring session.
     * This would be called during a match.
     * For the MVP, we'll just run a single ping.
     */
    suspend fun startPingMonitoring(sessionId: Long) {
        // For now, just a placeholder.
        // In a future version, this could run pings every 2 seconds.
    }

    // ============ DATA EXPORT/IMPORT ============

    /**
     * Export all touch events as a JSON string.
     */
    suspend fun exportTouchData(): String {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val events = touchEventDao.getAll().collect() // Can't collect here, need a different approach
            // Actually, we should use a snapshot query
            // For MVP: we'll just use a suspend function that returns a list
            // I'll add a non-flow function to the DAO
            // For now, return empty JSON
            "{}"
        }
    }
}

Wrapper for PermissionsX library to check all 4 dangerous permissions.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.permissions

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.VpnService
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment
import com.guolindev.permissionx.PermissionX
import com.guolindev.permissionx.callback.RequestCallback

/**
 * Helper class for managing the 4 dangerous permissions:
 * 1. Overlay (SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW)
 * 2. Accessibility (BIND_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE)
 * 3. VPN (BIND_VPN_SERVICE)
 * 4. DND (ACCESS_NOTIFICATION_POLICY)
 */
object PermissionHelper {

    // ============ OVERLAY PERMISSION ============

    /**
     * Check if overlay permission is granted.
     */
    fun hasOverlayPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)
    }

    /**
     * Request overlay permission.
     * Opens system settings.
     */
    fun requestOverlayPermission(activity: Activity) {
        val intent = Intent(
            Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION,
            android.net.Uri.parse("package:${activity.packageName}")
        )
        activity.startActivity(intent)
    }

    /**
     * Request overlay permission from fragment.
     */
    fun requestOverlayPermission(fragment: Fragment) {
        val intent = Intent(
            Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION,
            android.net.Uri.parse("package:${fragment.requireContext().packageName}")
        )
        fragment.startActivity(intent)
    }

    // ============ ACCESSIBILITY PERMISSION ============

    /**
     * Check if accessibility service is enabled for this app.
     */
    fun hasAccessibilityPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        val service = Settings.Secure.getString(
            context.contentResolver,
            Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES
        )
        return service?.contains(context.packageName) == true
    }

    /**
     * Request accessibility permission.
     * Opens system accessibility settings.
     */
    fun requestAccessibilityPermission(activity: Activity) {
        val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS)
        activity.startActivity(intent)
    }

    /**
     * Request accessibility permission from fragment.
     */
    fun requestAccessibilityPermission(fragment: Fragment) {
        val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS)
        fragment.startActivity(intent)
    }

    // ============ VPN PERMISSION ============

    /**
     * Check if VPN permission is granted.
     * This is done by preparing the VPN service.
     * If prepare() returns null, permission is granted.
     */
    fun hasVpnPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return VpnService.prepare(context) == null
    }

    /**
     * Request VPN permission.
     * This will show the system VPN permission dialog.
     * @return The intent to launch, or null if already granted.
     */
    fun requestVpnPermission(context: Context): Intent? {
        return VpnService.prepare(context)
    }

    /**
     * Request VPN permission from fragment.
     * @param fragment The fragment to launch from.
     * @param requestCode The request code for onActivityResult.
     */
    fun requestVpnPermission(fragment: Fragment, requestCode: Int) {
        val intent = VpnService.prepare(fragment.requireContext())
        if (intent != null) {
            fragment.startActivityForResult(intent, requestCode)
        }
    }

    // ============ DND PERMISSION ============

    /**
     * Check if DND (Notification Policy) permission is granted.
     */
    fun hasDndPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)
                as android.app.NotificationManager
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            notificationManager.isNotificationPolicyAccessGranted
        } else {
            true // Older versions don't have this restriction
        }
    }

    /**
     * Request DND permission.
     * Opens system notification policy settings.
     */
    fun requestDndPermission(activity: Activity) {
        val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_POLICY_ACCESS_SETTINGS)
        activity.startActivity(intent)
    }

    /**
     * Request DND permission from fragment.
     */
    fun requestDndPermission(fragment: Fragment) {
        val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_NOTIFICATION_POLICY_ACCESS_SETTINGS)
        fragment.startActivity(intent)
    }

    // ============ ALL PERMISSIONS CHECK ============

    /**
     * Check if all 4 permissions are granted.
     */
    fun hasAllPermissions(context: Context): Boolean {
        return hasOverlayPermission(context) &&
                hasAccessibilityPermission(context) &&
                hasVpnPermission(context) &&
                hasDndPermission(context)
    }

    /**
     * Get a map of all permission statuses.
     */
    fun getPermissionStatus(context: Context): Map<String, Boolean> {
        return mapOf(
            "overlay" to hasOverlayPermission(context),
            "accessibility" to hasAccessibilityPermission(context),
            "vpn" to hasVpnPermission(context),
            "dnd" to hasDndPermission(context)
        )
    }
}

Handles network operations: Ping, DNS, and connection checks.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.network

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.os.Build
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.SocketTimeoutException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * Network utility functions for ping tests, DNS checks, and connectivity.
 */
object NetworkUtils {

    /**
     * Perform a ping test to the specified host.
     * @param host The hostname or IP to ping (default: 8.8.8.8)
     * @param timeoutMs Timeout in milliseconds (default: 3000)
     * @return Ping in milliseconds, or -1 if failed.
     */
    suspend fun ping(host: String = "8.8.8.8", timeoutMs: Long = 3000): Int {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                val result = withTimeoutOrNull(timeoutMs) {
                    InetAddress.getByName(host).isReachable(timeoutMs.toInt())
                }
                val endTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                
                if (result == true) {
                    (endTime - startTime).toInt()
                } else {
                    -1
                }
            } catch (e: SocketTimeoutException) {
                -1
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                -1
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device has internet connectivity.
     */
    fun hasInternetConnection(context: Context): Boolean {
        val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE)
                as ConnectivityManager
        
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return false
            val caps = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return false
            return caps.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            val networkInfo = connectivityManager.activeNetworkInfo
            return networkInfo?.isConnectedOrConnecting == true
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current network type (Wi-Fi, Mobile, Ethernet, None).
     */
    fun getNetworkType(context: Context): String {
        val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE)
                as ConnectivityManager
        
        val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return "None"
        val caps = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return "None"

        return when {
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) -> "Wi-Fi"
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) -> "Mobile Data"
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_ETHERNET) -> "Ethernet"
            caps.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_BLUETOOTH) -> "Bluetooth"
            else -> "Unknown"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current network quality (0=poor, 1=fair, 2=good, 3=excellent).
     */
    fun getNetworkQuality(context: Context): Int {
        val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE)
                as ConnectivityManager
        
        val network = connectivityManager.activeNetwork ?: return 0
        val caps = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return 0

        val downSpeed = caps.linkDownstreamBandwidthKbps
        return when {
            downSpeed >= 100000 -> 3 // Excellent (>100 Mbps)
            downSpeed >= 20000 -> 2  // Good (>20 Mbps)
            downSpeed >= 5000 -> 1   // Fair (>5 Mbps)
            else -> 0                // Poor
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the network quality as a human-readable string.
     */
    fun getNetworkQualityText(context: Context): String {
        return when (getNetworkQuality(context)) {
            3 -> "Excellent"
            2 -> "Good"
            1 -> "Fair"
            else -> "Poor"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current IP address (for debugging).
     */
    suspend fun getCurrentIp(): String? {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                InetAddress.getLocalHost().hostAddress
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                null
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Perform a DNS lookup on a hostname.
     * @return The resolved IP address, or null if failed.
     */
    suspend fun dnsLookup(hostname: String): String? {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                InetAddress.getByName(hostname).hostAddress
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                null
            }
        }
    }
}

Advanced packet interceptor for VPN traffic. (Placeholder for future expansion.)

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.network

import java.net.InetAddress
import java.nio.ByteBuffer

/**
 * Advanced packet interceptor for the VPN service.
 * Parses IP packets, identifies UDP/TCP, and can manipulate traffic.
 * 
 * NOTE: This is a placeholder for future enhancements.
 * For MVP, the GameVpnService handles basic UDP prioritization.
 */
object TrafficInterceptor {

    /**
     * Parse an IP packet and extract protocol and port information.
     * @param packet The raw byte array from the VPN tunnel.
     * @return PacketInfo containing protocol, source/dest ports, or null if invalid.
     */
    fun parsePacket(packet: ByteArray): PacketInfo? {
        if (packet.size < 20) return null // Minimum IP header size
        
        // IP version is in the first byte (high nibble)
        val version = (packet[0].toInt() and 0xF0) shr 4
        if (version != 4) return null // Only IPv4 for now
        
        // IP header length (IHL) is the low nibble of the first byte
        val ihl = (packet[0].toInt() and 0x0F) * 4
        if (packet.size < ihl + 8) return null
        
        // Protocol is at byte 9 (offset 9)
        val protocol = packet[9].toInt() and 0xFF
        
        // Source and destination IP addresses
        val srcIp = getIpAddress(packet, 12)
        val destIp = getIpAddress(packet, 16)
        
        // For TCP/UDP, ports are after the IP header
        if (protocol == 6 || protocol == 17) {
            val srcPort = ((packet[ihl].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8) or (packet[ihl + 1].toInt() and 0xFF)
            val destPort = ((packet[ihl + 2].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8) or (packet[ihl + 3].toInt() and 0xFF)
            
            return PacketInfo(
                srcIp = srcIp,
                destIp = destIp,
                srcPort = srcPort,
                destPort = destPort,
                protocol = if (protocol == 6) "TCP" else "UDP",
                isUdp = protocol == 17
            )
        }
        
        return PacketInfo(
            srcIp = srcIp,
            destIp = destIp,
            srcPort = 0,
            destPort = 0,
            protocol = "Other",
            isUdp = false
        )
    }

    /**
     * Extract IP address from a byte array at a given offset.
     */
    private fun getIpAddress(packet: ByteArray, offset: Int): String {
        return "${packet[offset].toInt() and 0xFF}.${packet[offset + 1].toInt() and 0xFF}.${packet[offset + 2].toInt() and 0xFF}.${packet[offset + 3].toInt() and 0xFF}"
    }

    /**
     * Check if a packet is destined for Konami's servers (PES).
     * This is a placeholder - we don't have Konami's exact IPs.
     */
    fun isPesTraffic(packetInfo: PacketInfo): Boolean {
        // In a real implementation, you'd have a list of Konami's server IPs
        // For now, we just return true for all UDP traffic
        return packetInfo.isUdp
    }

    /**
     * Data class representing parsed packet information.
     */
    data class PacketInfo(
        val srcIp: String,
        val destIp: String,
        val srcPort: Int,
        val destPort: Int,
        val protocol: String,
        val isUdp: Boolean
    )

    /**
     * Priority class for packet prioritization.
     */
    enum class Priority {
        HIGH,      // UDP gameplay packets
        NORMAL,    // TCP packets
        LOW        // Non-game traffic (blocked)
    }

    /**
     * Determine the priority of a packet.
     */
    fun getPriority(packetInfo: PacketInfo): Priority {
        return if (packetInfo.isUdp) {
            Priority.HIGH
        } else {
            Priority.NORMAL
        }
    }
}
File 36: java/com/fspedx/booster/utils/performance/RamCleaner.kt
Handles RAM cleaning, 80% priority lock, and app whitelist management.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants

/**
 * RAM management utilities.
 * Handles force-stopping background apps and tracking memory usage.
 */
object RamCleaner {

    /**
     * Force-clean background apps to free RAM.
     * @param context Application context.
     * @param whitelist Set of package names to protect from killing.
     * @return MB of RAM freed.
     */
    fun forceClean(context: Context, whitelist: Set<String>): Int {
        val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
        val packageManager = context.packageManager

        // Get running app processes
        val runningProcesses = activityManager.runningAppProcesses ?: return 0
        
        val memoryBefore = getFreeRam(context)
        
        // Iterate and kill non-whitelisted apps
        for (process in runningProcesses) {
            val packageName = process.processName ?: continue
            
            // Skip whitelisted apps
            if (whitelist.contains(packageName)) continue
            
            // Skip the current app (FSPEDX) and system apps
            if (packageName == context.packageName) continue
            if (packageName.startsWith("android.")) continue
            if (packageName.startsWith("com.android.")) continue
            if (packageName.startsWith("com.google.android.")) continue
            
            // Skip PES itself
            if (packageName == Constants.PES_PACKAGE_NAME) continue
            if (packageName == Constants.PES_PACKAGE_NAME_ALT) continue
            
            try {
                // Get app info to check if it's a game or user app
                val appInfo = packageManager.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
                
                // Don't kill system apps
                if (appInfo.flags and android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo.FLAG_SYSTEM != 0) continue
                
                // Force stop the app
                activityManager.killBackgroundProcesses(packageName)
                
            } catch (e: PackageManager.NameNotFoundException) {
                // App not found - skip
            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
                // Can't kill this app - skip
            }
        }
        
        val memoryAfter = getFreeRam(context)
        return memoryAfter - memoryBefore
    }

    /**
     * Get current free RAM in MB.
     */
    fun getFreeRam(context: Context): Int {
        val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
        val memoryInfo = ActivityManager.MemoryInfo()
        activityManager.getMemoryInfo(memoryInfo)
        return (memoryInfo.availMem / (1024 * 1024)).toInt()
    }

    /**
     * Get total and used RAM in MB.
     * @return Pair(usedMB, totalMB)
     */
    fun getRamUsage(context: Context): Pair<Int, Int> {
        val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
        val memoryInfo = ActivityManager.MemoryInfo()
        activityManager.getMemoryInfo(memoryInfo)
        
        val totalMB = (memoryInfo.totalMem / (1024 * 1024)).toInt()
        val availMB = (memoryInfo.availMem / (1024 * 1024)).toInt()
        val usedMB = totalMB - availMB
        
        return Pair(usedMB, totalMB)
    }

    /**
     * Get current RAM usage as a percentage (0-100).
     */
    fun getRamUsagePercent(context: Context): Int {
        val (used, total) = getRamUsage(context)
        return if (total > 0) (used * 100 / total) else 0
    }

    /**
     * Check if RAM usage is above the 80% threshold.
     */
    fun isRamCritical(context: Context): Boolean {
        val (used, total) = getRamUsage(context)
        return used.toFloat() / total.toFloat() >= 0.8f
    }

    /**
     * Get a list of currently running apps (for debugging/display).
     */
    fun getRunningApps(context: Context): List<String> {
        val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
        val runningProcesses = activityManager.runningAppProcesses ?: return emptyList()
        return runningProcesses.mapNotNull { it.processName }
    }

    /**
     * Check if an app is running in the background.
     */
    fun isAppRunning(context: Context, packageName: String): Boolean {
        val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
        val runningProcesses = activityManager.runningAppProcesses ?: return false
        return runningProcesses.any { it.processName == packageName }
    }
}
File 37: java/com/fspedx/booster/utils/performance/ThermalUtils.kt
Reads battery and CPU temperature, and battery level.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.os.BatteryManager
import com.fspedx.booster.utils.constants.Constants
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.FileReader

/**
 * Thermal management utilities.
 * Reads battery temperature, CPU temperature, and battery level.
 */
object ThermalUtils {

    /**
     * Get the battery temperature in Celsius.
     */
    fun getBatteryTemperature(context: Context): Double {
        val batteryIntent = context.registerReceiver(
            null,
            IntentFilter(Intent.ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED)
        )
        if (batteryIntent == null) return 0.0
        
        val temp = batteryIntent.getIntExtra(BatteryManager.EXTRA_TEMPERATURE, 0)
        return temp / 10.0 // Temperature is in tenths of a degree Celsius
    }

    /**
     * Get the battery level as a percentage (0-100).
     */
    fun getBatteryLevel(context: Context): Int {
        val batteryIntent = context.registerReceiver(
            null,
            IntentFilter(Intent.ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED)
        )
        if (batteryIntent == null) return 0
        
        val level = batteryIntent.getIntExtra(BatteryManager.EXTRA_LEVEL, 0)
        val scale = batteryIntent.getIntExtra(BatteryManager.EXTRA_SCALE, 100)
        return (level * 100 / scale)
    }

    /**
     * Get the CPU temperature (may not be available on all devices).
     * @return Temperature in Celsius, or 0.0 if not available.
     */
    fun getCpuTemperature(): Double {
        // Common thermal zone paths
        val thermalPaths = listOf(
            "/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp",
            "/sys/devices/virtual/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp",
            "/sys/class/hwmon/hwmon0/temp1_input"
        )
        
        for (path in thermalPaths) {
            try {
                val reader = BufferedReader(FileReader(path))
                val tempStr = reader.readLine()
                reader.close()
                
                val temp = tempStr.toDoubleOrNull() ?: continue
                // Some zones report in millidegrees, some in degrees
                return if (temp > 1000) temp / 1000.0 else temp
                
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // File not found or unreadable - continue
            }
        }
        return 0.0
    }

    /**
     * Get the thermal status as an integer.
     * 0 = Normal, 1 = Warning, 2 = Critical
     */
    fun getThermalStatus(context: Context): Int {
        val temp = getBatteryTemperature(context)
        return when {
            temp >= Constants.TEMP_CRITICAL_THRESHOLD -> 2 // Critical
            temp >= Constants.TEMP_WARNING_THRESHOLD -> 1 // Warning
            else -> 0 // Normal
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get thermal status as a human-readable string.
     */
    fun getThermalStatusText(context: Context): String {
        return when (getThermalStatus(context)) {
            0 -> "Normal ✅"
            1 -> "Warming ⚠️"
            2 -> "Critical 🔥"
            else -> "Unknown"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device is overheating (critical threshold).
     */
    fun isOverheating(context: Context): Boolean {
        return getThermalStatus(context) >= 2
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device is warming up (warning threshold).
     */
    fun isWarming(context: Context): Boolean {
        return getThermalStatus(context) >= 1
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device is charging.
     */
    fun isCharging(context: Context): Boolean {
        val batteryIntent = context.registerReceiver(
            null,
            IntentFilter(Intent.ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED)
        )
        if (batteryIntent == null) return false
        
        val status = batteryIntent.getIntExtra(BatteryManager.EXTRA_STATUS, -1)
        return status == BatteryManager.BATTERY_STATUS_CHARGING ||
                status == BatteryManager.BATTERY_STATUS_FULL
    }

    /**
     * Get battery voltage (millivolts).
     */
    fun getBatteryVoltage(context: Context): Int {
        val batteryIntent = context.registerReceiver(
            null,
            IntentFilter(Intent.ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED)
        )
        return batteryIntent?.getIntExtra(BatteryManager.EXTRA_VOLTAGE, 0) ?: 0
    }
}

Switches between 120Hz and 60Hz refresh rates.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.performance

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings
import android.view.WindowManager
import java.lang.reflect.Method

/**
 * Utilities for switching screen refresh rates (120Hz ↔ 60Hz).
 * Uses reflection to access system settings.
 */
object RefreshRateUtils {

    /**
     * Set the screen refresh rate (in Hz).
     * @param context Application context.
     * @param targetRate Target refresh rate (60 or 120).
     * @return true if successful, false otherwise.
     */
    fun setRefreshRate(context: Context, targetRate: Int): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            return false // Not supported on older devices
        }

        // Only support 60Hz and 120Hz for now
        if (targetRate != 60 && targetRate != 120) {
            return false
        }

        return try {
            // Method 1: Use WindowManager's Display refresh rate (API 30+)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                val windowManager = context.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
                val display = windowManager.defaultDisplay
                val mode = display.mode
                val newMode = display.supportedModes.find { it.refreshRate >= targetRate }
                if (newMode != null && newMode != mode) {
                    // Actually, we can't change the mode directly without system permission.
                    // We'll use the settings method instead.
                }
            }

            // Method 2: Use System Settings (requires WRITE_SETTINGS permission)
            // Note: This doesn't actually work on all devices.
            // It's a placeholder for the actual implementation.
            // On Xiaomi/Redmi, you can use "peak_refresh_rate" or "user_refresh_rate"
            val result = Settings.System.putInt(
                context.contentResolver,
                "peak_refresh_rate",
                targetRate
            )
            
            // Alternative: "user_refresh_rate" for some devices
            if (!result) {
                Settings.System.putInt(
                    context.contentResolver,
                    "user_refresh_rate",
                    targetRate
                )
            }
            
            // Method 3: Use reflection to call hidden APIs
            // This is device-specific and may not work on all devices.
            // For Redmi 14C, we try the system property approach.
            
            // Try to set the system property (requires root - skip for now)
            // We'll just return true if we set the setting successfully.
            
            true
            
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the current refresh rate.
     * @return The refresh rate in Hz, or -1 if unknown.
     */
    fun getRefreshRate(context: Context): Int {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            return 60 // Default for older devices
        }

        return try {
            // Try to read from settings
            val peakRate = Settings.System.getInt(
                context.contentResolver,
                "peak_refresh_rate",
                60
            )
            if (peakRate > 0) {
                return peakRate
            }
            
            val userRate = Settings.System.getInt(
                context.contentResolver,
                "user_refresh_rate",
                60
            )
            if (userRate > 0) {
                return userRate
            }
            
            // Fallback: use display refresh rate
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                val windowManager = context.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
                return windowManager.defaultDisplay.refreshRate.toInt()
            }
            
            60
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            60
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device supports 120Hz refresh rate.
     */
    fun supports120Hz(context: Context): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            return false
        }
        
        return try {
            val windowManager = context.getSystemService(Context.WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
            val display = windowManager.defaultDisplay
            display.supportedModes.any { it.refreshRate >= 120 }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set refresh rate with a specific preference.
     * This is a more user-friendly method.
     */
    fun setPreferredRefreshRate(context: Context, use60Hz: Boolean) {
        val targetRate = if (use60Hz) 60 else 120
        setRefreshRate(context, targetRate)
    }
}
File 39: java/com/fspedx/booster/utils/ml/KMeansCluster.kt
K-Means clustering algorithm for button position suggestions.

kotlin
package com.fspedx.booster.utils.ml

import kotlin.math.sqrt
import kotlin.random.Random

/**
 * K-Means clustering implementation for touch data.
 * Groups touch points into clusters to find button centroids.
 */
object KMeansCluster {

    /**
     * Represents a touch point with X and Y coordinates.
     */
    data class TouchPoint(val x: Float, val y: Float)

    /**
     * Represents a cluster with a centroid and a list of points.
     */
    data class Cluster(val centroid: TouchPoint, val points: List<TouchPoint>)

    /**
     * Find K centroids from a list of touch points.
     * @param points List of touch points.
     * @param k Number of clusters (buttons) to find.
     * @param maxIterations Maximum iterations for convergence.
     * @param convergenceThreshold Distance threshold for convergence.
     * @return List of centroids (TouchPoint).
     */
    fun findCentroids(
        points: List<TouchPoint>,
        k: Int,
        maxIterations: Int = 100,
        convergenceThreshold: Float = 0.1f
    ): List<TouchPoint> {
        if (points.isEmpty() || k <= 0) {
            return emptyList()
        }

        if (k >= points.size) {
            return points.take(k)
        }

        // Initialize centroids using K-Means++ algorithm
        val centroids = mutableListOf<TouchPoint>()
        
        // First centroid: random point
        centroids.add(points[Random.nextInt(points.size)])
        
        // Remaining centroids: pick points with max distance from existing centroids
        for (i in 1 until k) {
            val distances = points.map { point ->
                centroids.minOf { centroid ->
                    distanceSquared(point, centroid)
                }
            }
            
            val totalDistance = distances.sum()
            if (totalDistance == 0f) break
            
            // Weighted random selection
            var randomValue = Random.nextFloat() * totalDistance
            var selectedIndex = 0
            for ((index, distance) in distances.withIndex()) {
                randomValue -= distance
                if (randomValue <= 0) {
                    selectedIndex = index
                    break
                }
            }
            
            centroids.add(points[selectedIndex])
        }

        // Run K-Means iterations
        var iteration = 0
        var hasConverged = false
        var previousCentroids = centroids.toList()

        while (iteration < maxIterations && !hasConverged) {
            // Assign points to nearest centroid
            val clusters = assignPointsToClusters(points, centroids)
            
            // Update centroids
            val newCentroids = clusters.map { cluster ->
                if (cluster.points.isEmpty()) {
                    // If cluster is empty, keep the old centroid
                    cluster.centroid
                } else {
                    val avgX = cluster.points.map { it.x }.average().toFloat()
                    val avgY = cluster.points.map { it.y }.average().toFloat()
                    TouchPoint(avgX, avgY)
                }
            }
            
            // Check convergence
            var totalMovement = 0f
            for (i in centroids.indices) {
                totalMovement += distance(centroids[i], newCentroids[i])
            }
            
            if (totalMovement < convergenceThreshold) {
                hasConverged = true
            }
            
            previousCentroids = centroids.toList()
            centroids.clear()
            centroids.addAll(newCentroids)
            iteration++
        }

        return centroids
    }

    /**
     * Assign each point to the nearest centroid.
     */
    private fun assignPointsToClusters(
        points: List<TouchPoint>,
        centroids: List<TouchPoint>
    ): List<Cluster> {
        val clusters = centroids.map { Cluster(it, mutableListOf()) }
        
        for (point in points) {
            var minDistance = Float.MAX_VALUE
            var closestIndex = 0
            
            for ((index, centroid) in centroids.withIndex()) {
                val dist = distanceSquared(point, centroid)
                if (dist < minDistance) {
                    minDistance = dist
                    closestIndex = index
                }
            }
            
            (clusters[closestIndex].points as MutableList<TouchPoint>).add(point)
        }
        
        return clusters
    }

    /**
     * Calculate Euclidean distance between two points.
     */
    private fun distance(point1: TouchPoint, point2: TouchPoint): Float {
        return sqrt(distanceSquared(point1, point2))
    }

    /**
     * Calculate squared Euclidean distance between two points.
     * More efficient than distance() for comparisons.
     */
    private fun distanceSquared(point1: TouchPoint, point2: TouchPoint): Float {
        val dx = point1.x - point2.x
        val dy = point1.y - point2.y
        return dx * dx + dy * dy
    }

    /**
     * Find the optimal number of clusters using the Elbow method.
     * @param points List of touch points.
     * @param maxK Maximum number of clusters to try.
     * @return The optimal K value.
     */
    fun findOptimalK(points: List<TouchPoint>, maxK: Int = 10): Int {
        if (points.isEmpty()) return 0
        
        val distortions = mutableListOf<Double>()
        
        for (k in 1..minOf(maxK, points.size)) {
            val centroids = findCentroids(points, k, maxIterations = 50)
            var distortion = 0.0
            
            for (point in points) {
                val minDist = centroids.minOf { centroid ->
                    distanceSquared(point, centroid).toDouble()
                }
                distortion += minDist
            }
            
            distortions.add(distortion)
        }
        
        // Find the "elbow" point
        if (distortions.size < 2) return 1
        
        // Calculate the rate of change
        val rates = mutableListOf<Double>()
        for (i in 1 until distortions.size) {
            val rate = (distortions[i - 1] - distortions[i]) / (distortions[0] - distortions.last())
            rates.add(rate)
        }
        
        // Find where the rate drops below a threshold (simple heuristic)
        val threshold = 0.5
        for ((index, rate) in rates.withIndex()) {
            if (rate < threshold) {
                return index + 1
            }
        }
        
        return if (distortions.size > 4) 4 else distortions.size
    }
}
